- kind: parameter
  text: Demolition and dismantling works must follow strict procedures for masonry, concrete, steel, and timber
    structures, including specific safety requirements such as catch platforms capable of sustaining at least 610
    kg/m2 or 730 kg/m2 depending on the shed design and structure height.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jadd127836c529d89
  - Jbb8d649d38a176e5
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C2383238aeae06153
  citations:
  - id: Jadd127836c529d89
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4A.md
    okf_sha256: 520ac8689dbcfe1f3be2ed99dafda8715eb436313221c6ca77dd66080cd2f726
    heading: Clause 3 — Serviceable and Unserviceable Materials
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:
    - 29
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4a_demolition/demolition_clauses.json
    json_sha256: e15324023aacf8bda736e3cf21e3a65735deb4ba19d458a5cd2099534b681a2e
    json_pointer: /clauses/2/fragments/6
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 29,\n  \"text\": \"progressed to within three storey of ground level, catch platforms\
      \ will not be considered\\n             necessary.\\n               c)     Catch platforms shall not be less\
      \ than 1.5 m in width measured in a horizontal\\n               direction from the face of the structure and\
      \ shall consist of outriggers supported not more\\n             than 3 m apart. Planks shall be laid tight\
      \ together, without openings between them and\\n             the walls. Catch platforms shall be provided\
      \ with a continuous solid parapet along its\\n              outer edge of at least 1 m height. The parapet\
      \ may be constructed with the same material\\n            as the platform.\\n              d) Catch platform\
      \ shall be capable of sustaining a live load of not less than 610 kg/m2.\\n              e) Catch platforms\
      \ shall neither be used for storing of materials nor dumping of\\n                  materials.\\n\\n     \
      \        3.11   Demolition of different types of structures and elements\\n\\n             3.11.1 General.\
      \      Structures may be dealt with as masonry, concrete, steel and\\n              timber. The structures\
      \ or their elements shall be dealt with as below, in addition to other\\n             requirements as applicable.\\\
      n\\n            3.11.2 Masonry structures.  Jack Arches – Where tie rods are present between main\\n     \
      \       supporting beams, these should not be cut until after the arch or series of arches in the\\n     \
      \          floor have been removed. Particular care should be exercised and full examination of\\n       \
      \      structure be made before the demolition is commenced. The floor should be demolished\\n           \
      \    in strips parallel to the span of arch rings (at right angles to the main floor beam).\\n\\n\\n\\n  \
      \          Brick Arches\\n\\n              1)   As much dead load as possible may be removed provided it does\
      \ not interfere with\\n                 stability of main arch rings; it should be noted that the load carrying\
      \ capacity of many old\\n             arches relies on the filling between the spandrels. On no account should\
      \ the restraining\\n              influence of the abutments be removed before the dead load of the spandrel\
      \ fill and the\\n             arch rings are removed. The normal sequence of demolition includes the following:\\\
      n                           -    remove the spandrel filling down to the springing line,\\n              \
      \             -    remove the arch rings,\\n                           -    Remove the abutments.\\n\\n  \
      \            2)   A single span arch can be demolished, by hand, by cutting narrow segments\\n           \
      \   progressively from each springing parallel to the span of the arch, until the width of the\\n        \
      \     arch has been reduced to a minimum which can then collapse. Where it is impossible to\\n           \
      \   allow debris to fall to the ground below, centering designed to carry the load should be\\n          \
      \   erected and the arch demolished progressively. The design of the centering should make\\n            \
      \  appropriate allowance for impact.\\n              3)    Where  deliberate collapse  is  feasible the crown\
      \ may be broken working\\n              progressively from the edges to the center.\\n              4)   \
      \  In multi-span arches, before individual spans are removed, lateral restraint should\\n           be provided\
      \ at the springing level. Demolition may be proceeded as for a single span care\\n             being taken\
      \ to demolish the spandrels down to the springing line as the work proceeds.\\n\\n             3.11.3 Reinforced\
      \ concrete\\n\\n              a)  Before commencing demolition, the condition and position of reinforcement\
      \ and\\n                    possibility of lack of  its continuity should be ascertained. Demolition should\
      \ be\\n             commenced by removing partitions, non-load bearing cladding, etc. and similar non-\\n\
      \                   structural elements.\\n              b) Where hand demolition methods are used, the following\
      \ procedures should be used:\"\n}"

  - id: Jbb8d649d38a176e5
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4A.md
    okf_sha256: 520ac8689dbcfe1f3be2ed99dafda8715eb436313221c6ca77dd66080cd2f726
    heading: Clause 3 — Serviceable and Unserviceable Materials
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:
    - 26
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4a_demolition/demolition_clauses.json
    json_sha256: e15324023aacf8bda736e3cf21e3a65735deb4ba19d458a5cd2099534b681a2e
    json_pointer: /clauses/2/fragments/3
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 26,\n  \"text\": \"iii.  While the mechanical device is in operation no workmen shall\
      \ be allowed\\n                       to enter the building being demolished;\\n\\n                      \
      \  iv. The device shall be so located as to avoid falling debris; and\\n\\n                     v. The device\
      \ when being used shall not cause any damage to adjacent\\n                        structure, power line,\
      \ other services, etc.\\n\\n       3.7    Protection of the public\\n\\n       Protection of the public before\
      \ and during demolition is important and the following points\\n       should be kept in mind:\\n\\n     \
      \  a) Every sidewalk or road adjacent to the work shall be closed or protected. All main\\n           roads,\
      \ which are open to the public shall be kept open to the public clear and\\n          unobstructed at all\
      \ times.\\n       b)  Children and public shall be kept out of the building and the adjoining yards.\\n  \
      \      c)   If the structure to be demolished is more than two-storied or 7.5 m high, measured\\n        \
      \  from the sidewalk or street which cannot be closed or safely diverted, and the\\n           horizontal\
      \ distance from the inside edge of the side walk to the structure is 4.5 m or\\n            less, a substantial\
      \ side walk shed shall be constructed over the entire length of the\\n          sidewalk adjacent to the structure\
      \ of sufficient width with a view to accommodating\\n           the pedestrian  traffic without causing congestion.\
      \ The sidewalk shall be lighted\\n             sufficiently to ensure safety at all times.\\n       d) The\
      \ structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades, which shall be of\\n            plain MS Sheet\
      \ of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic enamel\\n           paint of approved color,\
      \ quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including\\n         maintenance of the same, duly cleaning\
      \ the same frequently, arrangement for blinker\\n             light as per requirement. During demolition\
      \ barricades has to be kept continuously.\\n         Removal of barricade will not be permitted till completion\
      \ of demolition, dismantling\\n            of properties.\\n       e) A toe board at least 1 m high above\
      \ the roof of the side walk shed shall be provided\\n         on the outside edge and ends of the sidewalk\
      \ shed. Such boards may be vertical or\\n            inclined outward at not more than 45 degrees.\\n    \
      \       f)  Except where the roof of a side walk shed solidly abuts the structure, the face of the\\n    \
      \      sidewalk shed towards the building shall be completely closed by providing sheeting\\n            \
      \      / planking to prevent falling material penetrating into the shed.\\n       g) The roof of the sidewalk\
      \ shed shall be capable of sustaining a load of 730 kg/m2.\\n          Only in exceptional cases, say due\
      \ to lack of other space, the storing of the material\\n         on a sidewalk shed may be permitted in which\
      \ case the shed shall be designed for a\\n          load of 1460 kg/m2. Roof of sidewalk shed shall be designed\
      \ taking into account the\\n          impact of the falling debris. By frequent removal of loads it shall\
      \ be ensured that the\\n        maximum load, at any time, on the roof of the shed is not more than 600 kg/m2.\
      \ The\\n           height of the sidewalk shed shall be such as to give minimum clearance of 2.4 m.\\n   \
      \    h) Sidewalk shed openings, for loading purposes, shall be kept closed at all times except\\n        \
      \   during actual loading operations.\\n            i)  The deck flooring of the sidewalk shed shall consist\
      \ of plank of not less than 50 mm\\n           thickness closely laid and deck made watertight.\\n       \
      \     j)   All members of the shed shall be adequately braced and connected to resist\\n          displacement\
      \ of members or distortion of framework.\\n        k) When the horizontal distance from the inside edge of\
      \ the sidewalk to the structure is\\n         more than 4.5 m and less than 7.5 m, a sidewalk shed or fence\
      \ may be built or in their\\n          place a substantial railing shall be constructed on the inside of the\
      \ sidewalk or\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: Mandatory safety appliances required for workmen during demolition include safety helmets conforming to
    IS 2925:1984, celluloid lens goggles, leather or rubber gloves, and safety belts when working at heights.
  evidence_ids:
  - J0e7ba085aa6e53a9
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C065efa723b419dc8
  citations:
  - id: J0e7ba085aa6e53a9
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4A.md
    okf_sha256: 520ac8689dbcfe1f3be2ed99dafda8715eb436313221c6ca77dd66080cd2f726
    heading: Clause 3 — Serviceable and Unserviceable Materials
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:
    - 25
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4a_demolition/demolition_clauses.json
    json_sha256: e15324023aacf8bda736e3cf21e3a65735deb4ba19d458a5cd2099534b681a2e
    json_pointer: /clauses/2/fragments/2
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 25,\n  \"text\": \"(j)  Construction sheds and toolboxes should be so located as\
      \ to protect workers from\\n                    injuries of falling objects, wall, etc.\\n               \
      \ (k) A warning device should be installed in the area to be used to warn the workers, in\\n             \
      \  case of danger.\\n                     (l)  Screens shall be placed, where necessary, to prevent flying\
      \ pieces from injuring the\\n                   fellow workmen.\\n            (m) No demolition work shall\
      \ be carried out during storm or heavy rain.\\n               (n) No demolition work shall be carried out\
      \ at night especially when the building or\\n                   structure to be demolished is in an inhabited\
      \ area.\\n               (o)  All necessary safety appliances shall be issued to the workmen and their use\\\
      n                  explained. It shall be ensured that the workers are using all the safety appliances\\n\
      \                  while at work. The safety appliances should be as follows:\\n\\n                     (1)\
      \    Safety helmets as per IS 2925: 1984;\\n                     (2)    Goggles made of celluloid lens to\
      \ be worn at the time of demolition of floors,\\n                             walls, tearing of plaster, etc.,\
      \ especially when equipment like jack hammers\\n                         are used for demolition work, to\
      \ protect the eyes from flying pieces, dust,\\n                                    dirt, etc. that may be\
      \ blown up by wind.\\n                     (3)    Leather or rubber gloves to be worn during demolition of\
      \ RCC work or\\n                       removing steel work, where the hands of workers are likely to be injured.\\\
      n                     (4)    Safety belts while working  at higher level to prevent  falling from the\\n \
      \                            structure.\\n\\n                (p) First-aid equipment shall be available at\
      \ all demolition works of any magnitude. Also,\\n               by prior arrangement, a qualified doctor be\
      \ available at call.\\n               (q) When there is a possibility of fire breaking out, appropriate portable\
      \ first-aid fire\\n                 appliances (see IS 2190: 1992) shall be kept at hand.\\n             \
      \     (r)  The removal of a member may weaken the side wall of an adjoining structure and to\\n          \
      \       prevent possible damage, these walls  shall be supported  until such time as\\n               permanent\
      \ protection is provided.  In case of any danger is anticipated to the\\n                  adjoining structure,\
      \ the same shall be got vacated to avoid any danger to human life.\\n                (s)  Ladders, when used,\
      \ shall conform to IS 3696 (Part 2): 1991. Ladders or their side\\n                      rails shall extend\
      \ not less than 1.0 m above the floor or platform to which the ladder\\n                 gives access. All\
      \ ladders shall be secured against slipping out at the bottom and\\n                  against movement in\
      \ any direction at the top.\\n                   (t)   All exterior wall openings which extend down to the\
      \ floor level shall be barricaded to\\n               a height not less than 1 m above the floor level. All\
      \ floor openings and shafts not\\n               meant as material chutes shall be floored over and endorsed\
      \ with ground rails and\\n                 toe boards.\\n               (u)  All existing fixtures/services\
      \ required during demolition operations shall be well\\n                 protected with substantial covering\
      \ to the satisfaction of the Authority.\\n                (v) When demolition is to be done by mechanical\
      \ means such as weight ball and power\\n                  shovels, the following additional precautions are\
      \ necessary:\\n\\n                                              i. The area shall be barricaded for a minimum\
      \ distance of 1 ½ times the\\n                            height of the wall.\\n\\n                      \
      \                         ii. The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades, which\\n   \
      \                            shall be of plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting,\\n \
      \                            with synthetic enamel paint of approved color, quality and brand, writing\\n\
      \                                  letter and logo of metro including maintenance of the same, duly cleaning\\\
      n                           the same frequently, arrangement for blinker light as per requirement.\\n    \
      \                      During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously. Removal of\\n           \
      \                barricade will not be permitted till completion of demolition, dismantling of\\n        \
      \                     properties\"\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 32,\n  \"text\": \"d) When cutting a hole in an intermediate floor, between the storage\
      \ floor and the\\n                 floor which is being demolished, makes the intermediate floor or any portion\
      \ of it\\n              unsafe, then such intermediate floor shall be properly shored.  It shall also be\\\
      n             ensured that the supporting walls are not kept without adequate lateral restraints.\\n\\n  \
      \     3.13  References\\n\\n       Other Indian Standards on the subject of safety of workers are as follows:\\\
      n\\n                 IS No.                                        Title\\n        3696 (Part 1): 1987   \
      \   Safety code of scaffolds and ladders Part 1 Scaffolds\\n\\n        4014 (Part 2): 1967     Code of practice\
      \ for steel tubular scaffolding ; Part 2 Safety\\n                                   regulations for scaffolding\\\
      n\\n         3764: 1992           Code of safety for excavation work (first revision)\\n\\n         7969:\
      \ 1975             Safety code for handling and storage of building materials.\\n\\n         13415: 1992 \
      \         Code  of  safety  for  protective  barriers  in and around\\n                                  \
      \   buildings.\\n\\n        13416 (Part-1): 1992    Recommendations   for  preventive  measures  against\\\
      n                               hazards at workplaces; Part 1 Falling material hazards\\n                \
      \                   prevention.\\n\\n        13416 (Part 2): 1982   Recommendations   for  preventive  measures\
      \  against\\n                               hazards at work places; Part 2 Fall prevention.\\n\\n        \
      \ 13430: 1992          Code of practice for safety during additional construction\\n                     \
      \       and alteration to existing buildings.\"\n}"

- kind: summary
  text: Volume 2 Part 4A establishes the technical baseline distinguishing dismantling as carefully taking down
    building components without damage and lowering them to the ground, versus demolition as breaking up components
    before removal up to ground or basement levels.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jcc639cb1bc46d174
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C7dfcaf3eccbb2f96
  citations:
  - id: Jcc639cb1bc46d174
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4A.md
    okf_sha256: 520ac8689dbcfe1f3be2ed99dafda8715eb436313221c6ca77dd66080cd2f726
    heading: 'Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4A: Specifications for Demolition/Dismantling
      Works'
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:
    - 23
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4a_demolition/demolition_clauses.json
    json_sha256: e15324023aacf8bda736e3cf21e3a65735deb4ba19d458a5cd2099534b681a2e
    json_pointer: /source_page_context/0
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 23,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"170\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n                     PART – 4: TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS\\\
      n\\n          PART – A – SPECIFICATIONS FOR DEMOLITION / DISMANTLING WORKS\\n\\n           1.  Dismantling.\
      \ The term “dismantling” implies carefully taking up or down and removing\\n          the building materials\
      \ without damaging them. The articles dismantled shall be lowered to\\n          the ground and not thrown.\
      \ Dismantling work shall cover complete removal of the existing\\n           structure or part of a work including\
      \ all relevant items as indicated or as directed, clearing the\\n            site, sorting out useful materials\
      \ and stacking them as described, and disposing of the\\n          unserviceable materials.\\n\\n        \
      \   2.  Demolition.  The term “demolition” implies breaking up the components of the structure\\n        \
      \   building and then taking the components up or down.  This shall consists of demolishing\\n         whole\
      \ or part of work including all relevant items as indicated or as directed, clearing the site,\\n        \
      \   sorting our useful materials and stacking them as directed and disposing of the unserviceable\\n     \
      \     materials and rubbish as directed.  The removal of overlaying or adjacent materials,  if\\n        \
      \  required for demolition of the structure shall be separately indicated. Unless otherwise\\n           specified,\
      \ the building/structure shall be dismantled/ demolished up to ground level or up to\\n        basement level.\\\
      n\\n           3.  Serviceable and unserviceable materials\\n          3.1 Inventory.    Before  dismantling/demolition\
      \  operations  are  undertaken  by  the\\n              contractor, inventory of all materials, fittings and\
      \ fixtures (except hidden materials) which\\n             are considered useful shall be made and signed by\
      \ the Employers representative and the\\n              contractor, wherever the operations are entrusted to\
      \ a contractor.\\n\\n          3.2 Serviceable materials.    Any material which is in the opinion of the Employer’s\\\
      n              representative could be reused or otherwise useful will be considered as serviceable. The\\\
      n             serviceable/salvageable items (for which ‘owner losing structure totally/partially’ has no\\\
      n               interest) will belong to the contractor, for which the contractor shall give credit to BMRCL\\\
      n           (Summary Sheet)\\n\\n          3.3 Unserviceable materials.  Any material declared by the Employer’s\
      \ representative are\\n          not serviceable shall be considered as unserviceable. A register shall be\
      \ opened at the work\\n            site to show day-to-day account of the turnout of salvaged materials. The\
      \ register shall also\\n           indicate whether dismantled materials are properly stacked or wasted. The\
      \ contractor shall\\n        be responsible for the safe custody of all serviceable materials.\\n\\n     \
      \     3.4 Hazards in demolition.     Demolition of any structure is, inherently, more hazardous\\n       \
      \  than the construction or erection of the same. From the point of view of safety, the conditions\\n    \
      \      usually encountered while dismantling a structure, whatever  its magnitude, do not lend\\n        \
      \ themselves to the degree of control possible in the construction operations, where more\\n          stable\
      \ conditions are generally obtainable.  It is  all the more imperative; therefore, that\\n         adequate\
      \ attention is paid to planning and execution of demolition work, in its various stages,\\n         so as\
      \ to minimize the risk of accidents and injuries to the personnel engaged in demolition\\n          operations.\
      \ It has therefore become necessary to lay down certain safety procedures, which\\n         along with a planned\
      \ programme could ensure adequate safety, particularly with the\\n         involvement  of management, supervisors\
      \ and workers. The demolition work  shall be\\n         preceded in such a way that:\\n\\n              a)\
      \       it causes least damage and nuisance to other parts of the same building, adjoining\\n            \
      \         building and the residents of the building and the members of the public ; and\\n\\n           \
      \   b)       it satisfies all safety requirements to avoid mishaps and accidents.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 20 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n\
      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 20 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        197.0399169921875,\n        74.04320526123047,\n        414.0657043457031,\n        84.39871978759766\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"PART – 4: TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99978637695312,\n        86.64318084716797,\n        499.0270690917969,\n\
      \        122.43863677978516\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \" \\nPART – A – SPECIFICATIONS\
      \ FOR DEMOLITION / DISMANTLING WORKS \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        89.99958038330078,\n\
      \        124.68309783935547,\n        539.2271118164062,\n        198.39842224121094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 5,\n      \"text\": \"1. Dismantling. The term “dismantling” implies carefully taking up or down and removing\
      \ \\nthe building materials without damaging them. The articles dismantled shall be lowered to \\nthe ground\
      \ and not thrown.  Dismantling work shall cover complete removal of the existing \\nstructure or part of a\
      \ work including all relevant items as indicated or as directed, clearing the \\nsite, sorting out useful\
      \ materials and stacking them as described, and disposing of the \\nunserviceable materials. \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99945068359375,\n        200.64288330078125,\n        539.2283325195312,\n\
      \        324.8782043457031\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \" \\n2. Demolition. \\\
      nThe term “demolition” implies breaking up the components of the structure \\nbuilding and then taking the\
      \ components up or down.  This shall consists of demolishing \\nwhole or part of work including all relevant\
      \ items as indicated or as directed, clearing the site, \\nsorting our useful materials and stacking them\
      \ as directed and disposing of the unserviceable \\nmaterials and rubbish as directed.  The removal of overlaying\
      \ or adjacent materials, if \\nrequired for demolition of the structure shall be separately indicated. Unless\
      \ otherwise \\nspecified, the building/structure shall be dismantled/ demolished up to ground level or up\
      \ to \\nbasement level. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00018310546875,\n        327.002685546875,\n\
      \        539.2271728515625,\n        400.7181701660156\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 7,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"3. Serviceable and unserviceable materials \\n3.1 Inventory. \\nBefore dismantling/demolition operations\
      \ are undertaken by the \\ncontractor, inventory of all materials, fittings and fixtures (except hidden materials)\
      \ which \\nare considered useful shall be made and signed by the Employers representative and the \\ncontractor,\
      \ wherever the operations are entrusted to a contractor. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n   \
      \     72.00022888183594,\n        402.9626770019531,\n        539.2291870117188,\n        476.67816162109375\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"3.2 Serviceable materials. \\nAny material which\
      \ is in the opinion of the Employer’s \\nrepresentative could be reused or otherwise useful will be considered\
      \ as serviceable. The \\nserviceable/salvageable items (for which ‘owner losing structure totally/partially’\
      \ has no \\ninterest) will belong to the contractor, for which the contractor shall give credit to BMRCL \\\
      n(Summary Sheet) \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        89.99933624267578,\n        478.80267333984375,\n\
      \        539.228759765625,\n        539.9181518554688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"3.3 Unserviceable materials. \\nAny material declared by the Employer’s representative are \\nnot serviceable\
      \ shall be considered as unserviceable. A register shall be opened at the work \\nsite to show day-to-day\
      \ account of the turnout of salvaged materials. The register shall also \\nindicate whether dismantled materials\
      \ are properly stacked or wasted. The contractor shall \\nbe responsible for the safe custody of all serviceable\
      \ materials. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        89.9995346069336,\n        542.1626586914062,\n\
      \        539.2291259765625,\n        691.7182006835938\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 10,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n3.4 Hazards in demolition. \\nDemolition of any structure is, inherently, more hazardous \\nthan the\
      \ construction or erection of the same. From the point of view of safety, the conditions \\nusually encountered\
      \ while dismantling a structure, whatever its magnitude, do not lend \\nthemselves to the degree of control\
      \ possible in the construction operations, where more \\nstable conditions are generally obtainable. It is\
      \ all the more imperative; therefore, that \\nadequate attention is paid to planning and execution of demolition\
      \ work, in its various stages, \\nso as to minimize the risk of accidents and injuries to the personnel engaged\
      \ in demolition \\noperations. It has therefore become necessary to lay down certain safety procedures, which\
      \ \\nalong with a planned programme could ensure adequate safety, particularly with the \\ninvolvement of\
      \ management, supervisors and workers. The demolition work shall be \\npreceded in such a way that: \\n\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        90.7197494506836,\n        693.962646484375,\n        93.78703308105469,\n\
      \        704.3181762695312\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99917602539062,\n        706.5626220703125,\n        539.2271728515625,\n \
      \       767.5581665039062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"a) \\nit causes least\
      \ damage and nuisance to other parts of the same building, adjoining \\nbuilding and the residents of the\
      \ building and the members of the public ; and \\n \\nb) \\nit satisfies all safety requirements to avoid\
      \ mishaps and accidents. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n      \
      \  24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"170\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

- kind: summary
  text: Before starting demolition, contractors must complete inventories signed with the employer's representative,
    establish unserviceable material registers, and implement stringent safety planning including emergency exits,
    walkways, 24-hour watchmen for dangerous sites, utility shutoffs, and continuous 16-gauge MS sheet barricading.

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 24,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"171\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n       3.5    Planning.    Before beginning the actual work\
      \ of demolition, the contractor shall\\n        carefully study the structure, which is to be pulled down\
      \ and also of its surroundings. This\\n        shall include the following:\\n\\n        a) The manner in\
      \ which the various parts of buildings are supported and how far the\\n          stage by stage demolition\
      \ would affect the safety of the rest of the same structure\\n         and the adjoining structure;\\n   \
      \     b) A definite plan and procedure of demolition work shall be prepared, taking into\\n          account\
      \ the loads on various structural parts and their supports;\\n        c)  Before commencement of each stage\
      \ of demolition, the supervisor shall brief the\\n         workmen in detail regarding the safety aspects\
      \ to be kept in view;\\n        d)  Ensure that the demolition conditions do not, at any stage, enhance the\
      \ nuisance\\n           value of demolition work on the use of adjacent buildings;\\n        e) No structure\
      \ or part of the structure or any floor or temporary support or scaffold, side\\n            wall or any device\
      \ for equipment shall be loaded in excess of the safe load bearing\\n            capacity, in its then existing\
      \ condition; and\\n           f)   Stairs and stair railings, passage ways and ladders shall be left in place\
      \ as long as\\n            possible. These should be maintained in a safe condition.\\n\\n       3.6    Precautions\
      \ and protective measures before starting demolition work\\n      The following precautions and protective\
      \ measures shall be taken before commencement\\n        of demolition work:\\n\\n        (a)   On every demolition\
      \ job, danger signals shall be conspicuously posted all around\\n       the structure and all doors, openings\
      \ giving access to structures shall be kept barricaded\\n        or manned except during the actual passage\
      \ of workmen or equipment. However,\\n        provision shall be made for at least two independent exits for\
      \ escape of workmen during\\n      any emergency.\\n        (b)   Walkways and passageways shall be provided\
      \ for the use of the workmen who\\n        shall be instructed to use them and all such walkways and passageways\
      \ shall be kept\\n       adequately lighted, free from all debris and other materials.\\n        (c)    Where\
      \ in any work of demolition it is imperative, because of existing danger, to\\n      ensure that no unauthorized\
      \ person shall enter the site of demolition outside working\\n       hours, watchmen shall be employed all\
      \ 24 hrs.in a day. In addition to watching the site\\n      he shall also be responsible for maintaining all\
      \ signs, notices, lights, barricades, etc.\\n       During nights, red lights shall be placed on or about\
      \ the barricades.\\n        (d)    The power on all electrical service lines shall be shutoff and all such\
      \ lines cut or\\n       disconnected at or outside the property line. The only exception would be any power\
      \ lines\\n       required for the demolition work itself.  Prior to cutting of/disconnecting such lines, the\\\
      n      necessary approval of the Authority shall be obtained and beneficiaries intimated in\\n      advance.\\\
      n        (e)    The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades, which shall be\\n        of\
      \ plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic enamel paint\\n        of approved\
      \  color,  quality and brand,  writing  letter and logo  of metro  including\\n      maintenance of the same,\
      \ duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker\\n         light as per requirement. During demolition\
      \ barricades has to be kept continuously.\\n      Removal of barricade will not be permitted  till completion\
      \ of demolition, dismantling of\\n       properties\\n\\n          (f)   All mains and meters of the building\
      \ shall be removed or protected from damage.\\n        (g)  All gas, water, steam and other service lines\
      \ shall be shutoff and capped or otherwise\\n            controlled at or outside the property line.\\n  \
      \      (h)  If a structure to be demolished has been partially wrecked by fire, explosion or other\\n    \
      \       catastrophe, the walls and damaged roofs shall be shored and braced suitably.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 21 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\
      \        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n\
      \        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section Page 21 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        72.0006103515625,\n        74.04320526123047,\n        539.2277221679688,\n        122.43863677978516\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"3.5 \\nPlanning. \\nBefore beginning the actual work\
      \ of demolition, the contractor shall \\ncarefully study the structure, which is to be pulled down and also\
      \ of its surroundings.  This \\nshall include the following:  \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        109.44058990478516,\n        124.80309295654297,\n        539.2276000976562,\n        135.1586151123047\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"a) The manner in which the various parts of buildings\
      \ are supported and how far the \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        109.44038391113281,\n   \
      \     137.403076171875,\n        539.22802734375,\n        173.0785369873047\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 5,\n      \"text\": \"stage by stage demolition would affect the safety of the rest of the same structure\
      \ \\nand the adjoining structure; \\nb) A definite plan and procedure of demolition work shall be prepared,\
      \ taking into \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        109.44035339355469,\n        175.322998046875,\n\
      \        539.2276000976562,\n        198.39842224121094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"account the loads on various structural parts and their supports; \\nc) Before commencement of each stage\
      \ of demolition, the supervisor shall brief the \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        109.44032287597656,\n\
      \        200.64288330078125,\n        539.2272338867188,\n        223.59837341308594\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 7,\n      \"text\": \"workmen in detail regarding the safety aspects to be kept in view; \\nd) Ensure that\
      \ the demolition conditions do not, at any stage, enhance the nuisance \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        109.44029235839844,\n        225.96282958984375,\n        539.2273559570312,\n        248.91831970214844\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"value of demolition work on the use of adjacent buildings;\
      \ \\ne) No structure or part of the structure or any floor or temporary support or scaffold, side \\n\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        109.44007873535156,\n        251.28277587890625,\n        539.2277221679688,\n\
      \        299.5581970214844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"wall or any device for\
      \ equipment shall be loaded in excess of the safe load bearing \\ncapacity, in its then existing condition;\
      \ and \\nf) \\nStairs and stair railings, passage ways and ladders shall be left in place as long as \\npossible.\
      \ These should be maintained in a safe condition. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.39771270751953,\n\
      \        301.80267333984375,\n        539.2271728515625,\n        716.9180908203125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 10,\n      \"text\": \" \\n3.6 \\nPrecautions and protective measures before starting demolition work \\\
      nThe following precautions and protective measures shall be taken before commencement \\nof demolition work:\
      \ \\n \\n(a) \\nOn every demolition job, danger signals shall be conspicuously posted all around \\nthe structure\
      \ and all doors, openings giving access to structures shall be kept barricaded \\nor manned except during\
      \ the actual passage of workmen or equipment. However, \\nprovision shall be made for at least two independent\
      \ exits for escape of workmen during \\nany emergency. \\n(b) \\nWalkways and passageways shall be provided\
      \ for the use of the workmen who \\nshall be instructed to use them and all such walkways and passageways\
      \ shall be kept \\nadequately lighted, free from all debris and other materials. \\n(c)  \\nWhere in any work\
      \ of demolition it is imperative, because of existing danger, to \\nensure that no unauthorized person shall\
      \ enter the site of demolition outside working \\nhours, watchmen shall be employed all 24 hrs.in a day. In\
      \ addition to watching the site \\nhe shall also be responsible for maintaining all signs, notices, lights,\
      \ barricades, etc. \\nDuring nights, red lights shall be placed on or about the barricades. \\n(d) \\nThe\
      \ power on all electrical service lines shall be shutoff and all such lines cut or \\ndisconnected at or outside\
      \ the property line. The only exception would be any power lines \\nrequired for the demolition work itself.\
      \  Prior to cutting of/disconnecting such lines, the \\nnecessary approval of the Authority shall be obtained\
      \ and beneficiaries intimated in \\nadvance. \\n(e) \\nThe structure to be demolished/dismantled must have\
      \ barricades, which shall be \\nof plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic\
      \ enamel paint \\nof approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including \\nmaintenance\
      \ of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker \\nlight as per requirement. During\
      \ demolition barricades has to be kept continuously. \\nRemoval of barricade will not be permitted till completion\
      \ of demolition, dismantling of \\nproperties \\n \\n(f) All mains and meters of the building shall be removed\
      \ or protected from damage. \\n(g) All gas, water, steam and other service lines shall be shutoff and capped\
      \ or otherwise \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.39801025390625,\n        719.2825927734375,\n\
      \        539.2247924804688,\n        742.2380981445312\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"controlled at or outside the property line. \\n(h) If a structure to be demolished has been partially\
      \ wrecked by fire, explosion or other \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        128.63726806640625,\n\
      \        744.6025390625,\n        515.1046752929688,\n        754.9580688476562\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 12,\n      \"text\": \"catastrophe, the walls and damaged roofs shall be shored and braced suitably. \\\
      n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n\
      \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"171\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n\
      }"

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 23,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"170\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n                     PART – 4: TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS\\\
      n\\n          PART – A – SPECIFICATIONS FOR DEMOLITION / DISMANTLING WORKS\\n\\n           1.  Dismantling.\
      \ The term “dismantling” implies carefully taking up or down and removing\\n          the building materials\
      \ without damaging them. The articles dismantled shall be lowered to\\n          the ground and not thrown.\
      \ Dismantling work shall cover complete removal of the existing\\n           structure or part of a work including\
      \ all relevant items as indicated or as directed, clearing the\\n            site, sorting out useful materials\
      \ and stacking them as described, and disposing of the\\n          unserviceable materials.\\n\\n        \
      \   2.  Demolition.  The term “demolition” implies breaking up the components of the structure\\n        \
      \   building and then taking the components up or down.  This shall consists of demolishing\\n         whole\
      \ or part of work including all relevant items as indicated or as directed, clearing the site,\\n        \
      \   sorting our useful materials and stacking them as directed and disposing of the unserviceable\\n     \
      \     materials and rubbish as directed.  The removal of overlaying or adjacent materials,  if\\n        \
      \  required for demolition of the structure shall be separately indicated. Unless otherwise\\n           specified,\
      \ the building/structure shall be dismantled/ demolished up to ground level or up to\\n        basement level.\\\
      n\\n           3.  Serviceable and unserviceable materials\\n          3.1 Inventory.    Before  dismantling/demolition\
      \  operations  are  undertaken  by  the\\n              contractor, inventory of all materials, fittings and\
      \ fixtures (except hidden materials) which\\n             are considered useful shall be made and signed by\
      \ the Employers representative and the\\n              contractor, wherever the operations are entrusted to\
      \ a contractor.\\n\\n          3.2 Serviceable materials.    Any material which is in the opinion of the Employer’s\\\
      n              representative could be reused or otherwise useful will be considered as serviceable. The\\\
      n             serviceable/salvageable items (for which ‘owner losing structure totally/partially’ has no\\\
      n               interest) will belong to the contractor, for which the contractor shall give credit to BMRCL\\\
      n           (Summary Sheet)\\n\\n          3.3 Unserviceable materials.  Any material declared by the Employer’s\
      \ representative are\\n          not serviceable shall be considered as unserviceable. A register shall be\
      \ opened at the work\\n            site to show day-to-day account of the turnout of salvaged materials. The\
      \ register shall also\\n           indicate whether dismantled materials are properly stacked or wasted. The\
      \ contractor shall\\n        be responsible for the safe custody of all serviceable materials.\\n\\n     \
      \     3.4 Hazards in demolition.     Demolition of any structure is, inherently, more hazardous\\n       \
      \  than the construction or erection of the same. From the point of view of safety, the conditions\\n    \
      \      usually encountered while dismantling a structure, whatever  its magnitude, do not lend\\n        \
      \ themselves to the degree of control possible in the construction operations, where more\\n          stable\
      \ conditions are generally obtainable.  It is  all the more imperative; therefore, that\\n         adequate\
      \ attention is paid to planning and execution of demolition work, in its various stages,\\n         so as\
      \ to minimize the risk of accidents and injuries to the personnel engaged in demolition\\n          operations.\
      \ It has therefore become necessary to lay down certain safety procedures, which\\n         along with a planned\
      \ programme could ensure adequate safety, particularly with the\\n         involvement  of management, supervisors\
      \ and workers. The demolition work  shall be\\n         preceded in such a way that:\\n\\n              a)\
      \       it causes least damage and nuisance to other parts of the same building, adjoining\\n            \
      \         building and the residents of the building and the members of the public ; and\\n\\n           \
      \   b)       it satisfies all safety requirements to avoid mishaps and accidents.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 20 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n\

      \        122.43863677978516\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \" \\nPART – A – SPECIFICATIONS\

      \        124.68309783935547,\n        539.2271118164062,\n        198.39842224121094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 5,\n      \"text\": \"1. Dismantling. The term “dismantling” implies carefully taking up or down and removing\
      \ \\nthe building materials without damaging them. The articles dismantled shall be lowered to \\nthe ground\
      \ and not thrown.  Dismantling work shall cover complete removal of the existing \\nstructure or part of a\
      \ work including all relevant items as indicated or as directed, clearing the \\nsite, sorting out useful\
      \ materials and stacking them as described, and disposing of the \\nunserviceable materials. \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99945068359375,\n        200.64288330078125,\n        539.2283325195312,\n\
      \        324.8782043457031\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \" \\n2. Demolition. \\\
      nThe term “demolition” implies breaking up the components of the structure \\nbuilding and then taking the\
      \ components up or down.  This shall consists of demolishing \\nwhole or part of work including all relevant\
      \ items as indicated or as directed, clearing the site, \\nsorting our useful materials and stacking them\
      \ as directed and disposing of the unserviceable \\nmaterials and rubbish as directed.  The removal of overlaying\
      \ or adjacent materials, if \\nrequired for demolition of the structure shall be separately indicated. Unless\
      \ otherwise \\nspecified, the building/structure shall be dismantled/ demolished up to ground level or up\
      \ to \\nbasement level. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00018310546875,\n        327.002685546875,\n\

      : \"3. Serviceable and unserviceable materials \\n3.1 Inventory. \\nBefore dismantling/demolition operations\
      \ are undertaken by the \\ncontractor, inventory of all materials, fittings and fixtures (except hidden materials)\

      \ has no \\ninterest) will belong to the contractor, for which the contractor shall give credit to BMRCL \\\

      : \"3.3 Unserviceable materials. \\nAny material declared by the Employer’s representative are \\nnot serviceable\
      \ shall be considered as unserviceable. A register shall be opened at the work \\nsite to show day-to-day\

      \        539.2291259765625,\n        691.7182006835938\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 10,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n3.4 Hazards in demolition. \\nDemolition of any structure is, inherently, more hazardous \\nthan the\
      \ construction or erection of the same. From the point of view of safety, the conditions \\nusually encountered\

      \ in demolition \\noperations. It has therefore become necessary to lay down certain safety procedures, which\
      \ \\nalong with a planned programme could ensure adequate safety, particularly with the \\ninvolvement of\
      \ management, supervisors and workers. The demolition work shall be \\npreceded in such a way that: \\n\"\n\

    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 29,\n  \"text\": \"progressed to within three storey of ground level, catch platforms\
      \ will not be considered\\n             necessary.\\n               c)     Catch platforms shall not be less\
      \ than 1.5 m in width measured in a horizontal\\n               direction from the face of the structure and\
      \ shall consist of outriggers supported not more\\n             than 3 m apart. Planks shall be laid tight\
      \ together, without openings between them and\\n             the walls. Catch platforms shall be provided\
      \ with a continuous solid parapet along its\\n              outer edge of at least 1 m height. The parapet\
      \ may be constructed with the same material\\n            as the platform.\\n              d) Catch platform\
      \ shall be capable of sustaining a live load of not less than 610 kg/m2.\\n              e) Catch platforms\
      \ shall neither be used for storing of materials nor dumping of\\n                  materials.\\n\\n     \
      \        3.11   Demolition of different types of structures and elements\\n\\n             3.11.1 General.\
      \      Structures may be dealt with as masonry, concrete, steel and\\n              timber. The structures\
      \ or their elements shall be dealt with as below, in addition to other\\n             requirements as applicable.\\\
      n\\n            3.11.2 Masonry structures.  Jack Arches – Where tie rods are present between main\\n     \
      \       supporting beams, these should not be cut until after the arch or series of arches in the\\n     \
      \          floor have been removed. Particular care should be exercised and full examination of\\n       \
      \      structure be made before the demolition is commenced. The floor should be demolished\\n           \
      \    in strips parallel to the span of arch rings (at right angles to the main floor beam).\\n\\n\\n\\n  \
      \          Brick Arches\\n\\n              1)   As much dead load as possible may be removed provided it does\
      \ not interfere with\\n                 stability of main arch rings; it should be noted that the load carrying\
      \ capacity of many old\\n             arches relies on the filling between the spandrels. On no account should\
      \ the restraining\\n              influence of the abutments be removed before the dead load of the spandrel\
      \ fill and the\\n             arch rings are removed. The normal sequence of demolition includes the following:\\\
      n                           -    remove the spandrel filling down to the springing line,\\n              \
      \             -    remove the arch rings,\\n                           -    Remove the abutments.\\n\\n  \
      \            2)   A single span arch can be demolished, by hand, by cutting narrow segments\\n           \
      \   progressively from each springing parallel to the span of the arch, until the width of the\\n        \
      \     arch has been reduced to a minimum which can then collapse. Where it is impossible to\\n           \
      \   allow debris to fall to the ground below, centering designed to carry the load should be\\n          \
      \   erected and the arch demolished progressively. The design of the centering should make\\n            \
      \  appropriate allowance for impact.\\n              3)    Where  deliberate collapse  is  feasible the crown\
      \ may be broken working\\n              progressively from the edges to the center.\\n              4)   \
      \  In multi-span arches, before individual spans are removed, lateral restraint should\\n           be provided\
      \ at the springing level. Demolition may be proceeded as for a single span care\\n             being taken\
      \ to demolish the spandrels down to the springing line as the work proceeds.\\n\\n             3.11.3 Reinforced\
      \ concrete\\n\\n              a)  Before commencing demolition, the condition and position of reinforcement\
      \ and\\n                    possibility of lack of  its continuity should be ascertained. Demolition should\
      \ be\\n             commenced by removing partitions, non-load bearing cladding, etc. and similar non-\\n\
      \                   structural elements.\\n              b) Where hand demolition methods are used, the following\
      \ procedures should be used:\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: Catch platforms must be at least 1.5 meters wide in a horizontal direction, supported on outriggers spaced
    not more than 3 meters apart with tight planks, feature a continuous solid 1-meter high outer parapet, and be
    capable of sustaining a live load of at least 610 kg/m2 without being used for storage or debris dumping.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jadd127836c529d89
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C9656df61ece89106
  citations:
  - id: Jadd127836c529d89
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4A.md
    okf_sha256: 520ac8689dbcfe1f3be2ed99dafda8715eb436313221c6ca77dd66080cd2f726
    heading: Clause 3 — Serviceable and Unserviceable Materials
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:
    - 29
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4a_demolition/demolition_clauses.json
    json_sha256: e15324023aacf8bda736e3cf21e3a65735deb4ba19d458a5cd2099534b681a2e
    json_pointer: /clauses/2/fragments/6
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 29,\n  \"text\": \"progressed to within three storey of ground level, catch platforms\
      \ will not be considered\\n             necessary.\\n               c)     Catch platforms shall not be less\
      \ than 1.5 m in width measured in a horizontal\\n               direction from the face of the structure and\
      \ shall consist of outriggers supported not more\\n             than 3 m apart. Planks shall be laid tight\
      \ together, without openings between them and\\n             the walls. Catch platforms shall be provided\
      \ with a continuous solid parapet along its\\n              outer edge of at least 1 m height. The parapet\
      \ may be constructed with the same material\\n            as the platform.\\n              d) Catch platform\
      \ shall be capable of sustaining a live load of not less than 610 kg/m2.\\n              e) Catch platforms\
      \ shall neither be used for storing of materials nor dumping of\\n                  materials.\\n\\n     \
      \        3.11   Demolition of different types of structures and elements\\n\\n             3.11.1 General.\
      \      Structures may be dealt with as masonry, concrete, steel and\\n              timber. The structures\
      \ or their elements shall be dealt with as below, in addition to other\\n             requirements as applicable.\\\
      n\\n            3.11.2 Masonry structures.  Jack Arches – Where tie rods are present between main\\n     \
      \       supporting beams, these should not be cut until after the arch or series of arches in the\\n     \
      \          floor have been removed. Particular care should be exercised and full examination of\\n       \
      \      structure be made before the demolition is commenced. The floor should be demolished\\n           \
      \    in strips parallel to the span of arch rings (at right angles to the main floor beam).\\n\\n\\n\\n  \
      \          Brick Arches\\n\\n              1)   As much dead load as possible may be removed provided it does\
      \ not interfere with\\n                 stability of main arch rings; it should be noted that the load carrying\
      \ capacity of many old\\n             arches relies on the filling between the spandrels. On no account should\
      \ the restraining\\n              influence of the abutments be removed before the dead load of the spandrel\
      \ fill and the\\n             arch rings are removed. The normal sequence of demolition includes the following:\\\
      n                           -    remove the spandrel filling down to the springing line,\\n              \
      \             -    remove the arch rings,\\n                           -    Remove the abutments.\\n\\n  \
      \            2)   A single span arch can be demolished, by hand, by cutting narrow segments\\n           \
      \   progressively from each springing parallel to the span of the arch, until the width of the\\n        \
      \     arch has been reduced to a minimum which can then collapse. Where it is impossible to\\n           \
      \   allow debris to fall to the ground below, centering designed to carry the load should be\\n          \
      \   erected and the arch demolished progressively. The design of the centering should make\\n            \
      \  appropriate allowance for impact.\\n              3)    Where  deliberate collapse  is  feasible the crown\
      \ may be broken working\\n              progressively from the edges to the center.\\n              4)   \
      \  In multi-span arches, before individual spans are removed, lateral restraint should\\n           be provided\
      \ at the springing level. Demolition may be proceeded as for a single span care\\n             being taken\
      \ to demolish the spandrels down to the springing line as the work proceeds.\\n\\n             3.11.3 Reinforced\
      \ concrete\\n\\n              a)  Before commencing demolition, the condition and position of reinforcement\
      \ and\\n                    possibility of lack of  its continuity should be ascertained. Demolition should\
      \ be\\n             commenced by removing partitions, non-load bearing cladding, etc. and similar non-\\n\
      \                   structural elements.\\n              b) Where hand demolition methods are used, the following\
      \ procedures should be used:\"\n}"

  text: Volume 2 Part 4A details operational requirements and safety precautions for planning, public protection,
    sequence of operations, wall demolition, floor demolition, and protective structures during BMRCL Phase 3 demolition
    works.

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 26,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"173\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n\\n                                   iii.  While the mechanical\
      \ device is in operation no workmen shall be allowed\\n                       to enter the building being\
      \ demolished;\\n\\n                        iv. The device shall be so located as to avoid falling debris;\
      \ and\\n\\n                     v. The device when being used shall not cause any damage to adjacent\\n  \
      \                      structure, power line, other services, etc.\\n\\n       3.7    Protection of the public\\\
      n\\n       Protection of the public before and during demolition is important and the following points\\n\
      \       should be kept in mind:\\n\\n       a) Every sidewalk or road adjacent to the work shall be closed\
      \ or protected. All main\\n           roads, which are open to the public shall be kept open to the public\
      \ clear and\\n          unobstructed at all times.\\n       b)  Children and public shall be kept out of the\
      \ building and the adjoining yards.\\n        c)   If the structure to be demolished is more than two-storied\
      \ or 7.5 m high, measured\\n          from the sidewalk or street which cannot be closed or safely diverted,\
      \ and the\\n           horizontal distance from the inside edge of the side walk to the structure is 4.5 m\
      \ or\\n            less, a substantial side walk shed shall be constructed over the entire length of the\\\
      n          sidewalk adjacent to the structure of sufficient width with a view to accommodating\\n        \
      \   the pedestrian  traffic without causing congestion. The sidewalk shall be lighted\\n             sufficiently\
      \ to ensure safety at all times.\\n       d) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades,\
      \ which shall be of\\n            plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic\
      \ enamel\\n           paint of approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including\\\
      n         maintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker\\n         \
      \    light as per requirement. During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously.\\n         Removal\
      \ of barricade will not be permitted till completion of demolition, dismantling\\n            of properties.\\\
      n       e) A toe board at least 1 m high above the roof of the side walk shed shall be provided\\n       \
      \  on the outside edge and ends of the sidewalk shed. Such boards may be vertical or\\n            inclined\
      \ outward at not more than 45 degrees.\\n           f)  Except where the roof of a side walk shed solidly\
      \ abuts the structure, the face of the\\n          sidewalk shed towards the building shall be completely\
      \ closed by providing sheeting\\n                  / planking to prevent falling material penetrating into\
      \ the shed.\\n       g) The roof of the sidewalk shed shall be capable of sustaining a load of 730 kg/m2.\\\
      n          Only in exceptional cases, say due to lack of other space, the storing of the material\\n     \
      \    on a sidewalk shed may be permitted in which case the shed shall be designed for a\\n          load of\
      \ 1460 kg/m2. Roof of sidewalk shed shall be designed taking into account the\\n          impact of the falling\
      \ debris. By frequent removal of loads it shall be ensured that the\\n        maximum load, at any time, on\
      \ the roof of the shed is not more than 600 kg/m2. The\\n           height of the sidewalk shed shall be such\
      \ as to give minimum clearance of 2.4 m.\\n       h) Sidewalk shed openings, for loading purposes, shall be\
      \ kept closed at all times except\\n           during actual loading operations.\\n            i)  The deck\
      \ flooring of the sidewalk shed shall consist of plank of not less than 50 mm\\n           thickness closely\
      \ laid and deck made watertight.\\n            j)   All members of the shed shall be adequately braced and\
      \ connected to resist\\n          displacement of members or distortion of framework.\\n        k) When the\
      \ horizontal distance from the inside edge of the sidewalk to the structure is\\n         more than 4.5 m\
      \ and less than 7.5 m, a sidewalk shed or fence may be built or in their\\n          place a substantial railing\
      \ shall be constructed on the inside of the sidewalk or\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.\
      \                 September 2026           Section Page 23 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"\

      n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99766540527344,\n        187.92291259765625,\n        539.2254028320312,\n\
      \        261.6382751464844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"3.7 \\nProtection of the\
      \ public \\n \\nProtection of the public before and during demolition is important and the following points\
      \ \\nshould be kept in mind: \\n \\na) Every sidewalk or road adjacent to the work shall be closed or protected.\
      \ All main \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99786376953125,\n        263.88275146484375,\n\
      \        539.2250366210938,\n        312.1581726074219\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 10,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"roads, which are open to the public shall be kept open to the public clear and \\nunobstructed at all\
      \ times. \\nb) Children and public shall be kept out of the building and the adjoining yards. \\nc) If the\
      \ structure to be demolished is more than two-storied or 7.5 m high, measured \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n     \
      \ \"bbox\": [\n        107.99705505371094,\n        314.52264404296875,\n        539.2247314453125,\n    \
      \    400.7181091308594\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"from the sidewalk or street\
      \ which cannot be closed or safely diverted, and the \\nhorizontal distance from the inside edge of the side\
      \ walk to the structure is 4.5 m or \\nless, a substantial side walk shed shall be constructed over the entire\
      \ length of the \\nsidewalk adjacent to the structure of sufficient width with a view to accommodating \\\
      nthe pedestrian traffic without causing congestion. The sidewalk shall be lighted \\nsufficiently to ensure\
      \ safety at all times. \\nd) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades, which shall be\
      \ of \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99726867675781,\n        403.0826110839844,\n  \
      \      539.2247924804688,\n        489.2781066894531\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic enamel \\npaint of approved\
      \ color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including \\nmaintenance of the same, duly cleaning\
      \ the same frequently, arrangement for blinker \\nlight as per requirement. During demolition barricades has\
      \ to be kept continuously. \\nRemoval of barricade will not be permitted till completion of demolition, dismantling\
      \ \\nof properties. \\ne) A toe board at least 1 m high above the roof of the side walk shed shall be provided\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99745178222656,\n        491.5226135253906,\n     \
      \   539.2250366210938,\n        527.1981201171875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 13,\n      \"text\":\
      \ \"on the outside edge and ends of the sidewalk shed. Such boards may be vertical or \\ninclined outward\
      \ at not more than 45 degrees. \\nf) Except where the roof of a side walk shed solidly abuts the structure,\
      \ the face of the \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.9964599609375,\n        529.5626220703125,\n\
      \        542.343017578125,\n        565.1181030273438\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 14,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"sidewalk shed towards the building shall be completely closed by providing sheeting \\n/ planking to prevent\
      \ falling material penetrating into the shed. \\ng) The roof of the sidewalk shed shall be capable of sustaining\
      \ a load of 730 kg/m2.  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99543762207031,\n        567.4826049804688,\n\
      \        539.2232666015625,\n        653.6781005859375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 15,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"Only in exceptional cases, say due to lack of other space, the storing of the material \\non a sidewalk\
      \ shed may be permitted in which case the shed shall be designed for a \\nload of 1460 kg/m2.  Roof of sidewalk\
      \ shed shall be designed taking into account the \\nimpact of the falling debris. By frequent removal of loads\
      \ it shall be ensured that the \\nmaximum load, at any time, on the roof of the shed is not more than 600\
      \ kg/m2.  The \\nheight of the sidewalk shed shall be such as to give minimum clearance of 2.4 m. \\nh) Sidewalk\
      \ shed openings, for loading purposes, shall be kept closed at all times except \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n   \
      \   \"bbox\": [\n        107.99462890625,\n        656.0426025390625,\n        539.2217407226562,\n      \
      \  678.9981079101562\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"during actual loading operations.\
      \ \\ni) The deck flooring of the sidewalk shed shall consist of plank of not less than 50 mm \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.98941802978516,\n        681.362548828125,\n        539.2218627929688,\n\
      \        704.318115234375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"thickness closely laid\
      \ and deck made watertight. \\nj) All members of the shed shall be adequately braced and connected to resist\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99440002441406,\n        706.5625610351562,\n     \
      \   539.221435546875,\n        729.6381225585938\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \"\
      displacement of members or distortion of framework. \\nk) When the horizontal distance from the inside edge\
      \ of the sidewalk to the structure is \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        125.99459075927734,\n\
      \        731.882568359375,\n        539.22216796875,\n        754.9580688476562\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 19,\n      \"text\": \"more than 4.5 m and less than 7.5 m, a sidewalk shed or fence may be built or in\
      \ their \\nplace a substantial railing shall be constructed on the inside of the sidewalk or \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n\
