    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 28,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"175\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n       3.10   Demolition of walls\\n\\n       3.10.1 Procedure.\
      \   The following procedure shall be followed when demolishing walls:\\n\\n        a) While walls or sections\
      \ of masonry are being demolished  it shall be ensured that\\n           they are not allowed to fall as a\
      \ single mass on the floors of the building so as not to\\n          exceed the safe carrying capacity of\
      \ the floor; wherever practicable, they may fall\\n         away from the floors on to catch platforms. Overloading\
      \ of floors shall be prevented\\n          by removing the accumulating debris through chutes or by other\
      \ means immediately.\\n         The floor shall be inspected by the Authority before undertaking demolition\
      \ work and\\n                     if the same is found incapable of carrying the load of debris, necessary\
      \ precautions\\n             shall be taken to prevent any unexpected collapse of the floor.\\n        b)\
      \  Walls shall be removed part by part. Stages shall be provided for the men to work\\n           on, if the\
      \ walls are very thin and dangerous to work by standing over them.\\n         c) No section of the wall whose\
      \ height is more than 15 times the thickness, shall be\\n           permitted to stand without lateral bracing\
      \ unless such a wall is in good condition and\\n         was originally designed to stand without such lateral\
      \ bracing or support.\\n        d)  Structural or load supporting members on any floor shall not be removed\
      \ or cut until\\n                all the storey above that floor have been demolished and removed.\\n    \
      \    e)  Before demolishing any interior or exterior wall within 3 m of the opening in the floor\\n      \
      \     immediately below, such opening shall be substantially planked over, unless access\\n              \
      \ is denied to workmen to that portion of the floor immediately below the opening, in\\n           the floor\
      \ of the storey being demolished, where any debris passing through the\\n          opening may fall.\\n  \
      \          f)  In framed structures, if the structure is properly designed to the standards and to the\\n\
      \            specifications in such cases the frame may be left in position during demolition of\\n      \
      \    masonry work. Where this is done all beams, girders, etc., shall be cleared of all\\n           loose\
      \ materials as the demolition of masonry work progresses downward provided it\\n               is still strong\
      \ enough to stand as an independent structure.\\n\\n        g) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must\
      \ have barricades, which shall be of\\n             plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting,\
      \ with synthetic enamel\\n            paint of approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo\
      \ of metro including\\n          maintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for\
      \ blinker\\n              light as per requirement. During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously.\\\
      n         Removal of barricade will not be permitted till completion of demolition, dismantling\\n       \
      \      of properties.\\n        h) Walkways shall be provided to enable workmen to reach or leave their work\
      \ on any\\n            scaffold or wall. Such walkways shall neither be less than 3 planks wide, nor less\\\
      n           than 0.8 m in width.\\n             i)   After completion of each day’s work, all walls shall\
      \ be left stable to avoid any danger\\n             of getting overturned.\\n             j)  Foundation walls\
      \ which serve as retaining walls to support the earth or adjoining\\n             structure, shall not be\
      \ demolished until such an adjoining structure has been under\\n           pinned or braced and the earth\
      \ removed by sheet piling or sheathing.\\n\\n       3.10.2 Catch platforms\\n      Catch platforms shall be\
      \ provided in case of demolition of exterior walls in multi-storey\\n        buildings. The following details\
      \ may be considered:\\n\\n       a) Catch platforms shall generally be provided for multi-storied buildings\
      \ more than 20\\n    m high to prevent injuries to the worker and to the public when exterior walls are being\\\
      n       demolished.\\n       b)    Such platforms shall be constructed and maintained not more than three\
      \ storey\\n      below the storey from which the exterior wall is being demolished. When demolition has\\\
      n\\n\\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 25 of 37\"\
      ,\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n\

      : \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & \

      : 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section\

      \ to fall as a single mass on the floors of the building so as not to \\nexceed the safe carrying capacity\
      \ of the floor; wherever practicable, they may fall \\naway from the floors on to catch platforms. Overloading\
      \ of floors shall be prevented \\nby removing the accumulating debris through chutes or by other means immediately.\
      \  \\nThe floor shall be inspected by the Authority before undertaking demolition work and \\nif the same\

      \ unexpected collapse of the floor. \\nb) Walls shall be removed part by part. Stages shall be provided for\
      \ the men to work \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.56028747558594,\n        238.56280517578125,\n\

      \ such a wall is in good condition and \\nwas originally designed to stand without such lateral bracing or\
      \ support. \\nd) Structural or load supporting members on any floor shall not be removed or cut until \\n\"\

      \ floor have been demolished and removed. \\ne) Before demolishing any interior or exterior wall within 3\
      \ m of the opening in the floor \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.56047058105469,\n   \
      \     327.12261962890625,\n        539.2295532226562,\n        388.11810302734375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 10,\n      \"text\": \"immediately below, such opening shall be substantially planked over, unless access\
      \ \\nis denied to workmen to that portion of the floor immediately below the opening, in \\nthe floor of the\
      \ storey being demolished, where any debris passing through the \\nopening may fall. \\nf) In framed structures,\
      \ if the structure is properly designed to the standards and to the \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\":\
      \ [\n        108.00022888183594,\n        390.36260986328125,\n        539.228271484375,\n        451.35809326171875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"specifications in such cases the frame may be left\
      \ in position during demolition of \\nmasonry work.  Where this is done all beams, girders, etc., shall be\
      \ cleared of all \\nloose materials as the demolition of masonry work progresses downward provided it \\nis\
      \ still strong enough to stand as an independent structure. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        112.56022644042969,\n        453.60260009765625,\n        539.228759765625,\n        463.9580993652344\n\

      \ have barricades, which shall be of \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.55940246582031,\n\
      \        466.3226013183594,\n        539.2269897460938,\n        552.5181274414062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic enamel\
      \ \\npaint of approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including \\nmaintenance\
      \ of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker \\nlight as per requirement. During\
      \ demolition barricades has to be kept continuously. \\nRemoval of barricade will not be permitted till completion\
      \ of demolition, dismantling \\nof properties. \\nh) Walkways shall be provided to enable workmen to reach\

      \ \\nj) Foundation walls which serve as retaining walls to support the earth or adjoining \\n\"\n    },\n\

      : \" \\n3.10.2 Catch platforms  \\nCatch platforms shall be provided in case of demolition of exterior walls\
      \ in multi-storey \\nbuildings. The following details may be considered: \\n \\na) Catch platforms shall generally\
      \ be provided for multi-storied buildings more than 20 \\nm high to prevent injuries to the worker and to\

  - id: Jcc4871ddd5018819
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4A.md
    okf_sha256: 520ac8689dbcfe1f3be2ed99dafda8715eb436313221c6ca77dd66080cd2f726
    heading: 'Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4A: Specifications for Demolition/Dismantling

    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:

    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4a_demolition/demolition_clauses.json
    json_sha256: e15324023aacf8bda736e3cf21e3a65735deb4ba19d458a5cd2099534b681a2e
    json_pointer: /source_page_context/2
    json_record_id: null

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 25,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"172\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n                     (j)  Construction sheds and toolboxes\
      \ should be so located as to protect workers from\\n                    injuries of falling objects, wall,\
      \ etc.\\n                (k) A warning device should be installed in the area to be used to warn the workers,\
      \ in\\n               case of danger.\\n                     (l)  Screens shall be placed, where necessary,\
      \ to prevent flying pieces from injuring the\\n                   fellow workmen.\\n            (m) No demolition\
      \ work shall be carried out during storm or heavy rain.\\n               (n) No demolition work shall be carried\
      \ out at night especially when the building or\\n                   structure to be demolished is in an inhabited\
      \ area.\\n               (o)  All necessary safety appliances shall be issued to the workmen and their use\\\
      n                  explained. It shall be ensured that the workers are using all the safety appliances\\n\
      \                  while at work. The safety appliances should be as follows:\\n\\n                     (1)\
      \    Safety helmets as per IS 2925: 1984;\\n                     (2)    Goggles made of celluloid lens to\
      \ be worn at the time of demolition of floors,\\n                             walls, tearing of plaster, etc.,\
      \ especially when equipment like jack hammers\\n                         are used for demolition work, to\
      \ protect the eyes from flying pieces, dust,\\n                                    dirt, etc. that may be\
      \ blown up by wind.\\n                     (3)    Leather or rubber gloves to be worn during demolition of\
      \ RCC work or\\n                       removing steel work, where the hands of workers are likely to be injured.\\\
      n                     (4)    Safety belts while working  at higher level to prevent  falling from the\\n \
      \                            structure.\\n\\n                (p) First-aid equipment shall be available at\
      \ all demolition works of any magnitude. Also,\\n               by prior arrangement, a qualified doctor be\
      \ available at call.\\n               (q) When there is a possibility of fire breaking out, appropriate portable\
      \ first-aid fire\\n                 appliances (see IS 2190: 1992) shall be kept at hand.\\n             \
      \     (r)  The removal of a member may weaken the side wall of an adjoining structure and to\\n          \
      \       prevent possible damage, these walls  shall be supported  until such time as\\n               permanent\
      \ protection is provided.  In case of any danger is anticipated to the\\n                  adjoining structure,\
      \ the same shall be got vacated to avoid any danger to human life.\\n                (s)  Ladders, when used,\
      \ shall conform to IS 3696 (Part 2): 1991. Ladders or their side\\n                      rails shall extend\
      \ not less than 1.0 m above the floor or platform to which the ladder\\n                 gives access. All\
      \ ladders shall be secured against slipping out at the bottom and\\n                  against movement in\
      \ any direction at the top.\\n                   (t)   All exterior wall openings which extend down to the\
      \ floor level shall be barricaded to\\n               a height not less than 1 m above the floor level. All\
      \ floor openings and shafts not\\n               meant as material chutes shall be floored over and endorsed\
      \ with ground rails and\\n                 toe boards.\\n               (u)  All existing fixtures/services\
      \ required during demolition operations shall be well\\n                 protected with substantial covering\
      \ to the satisfaction of the Authority.\\n                (v) When demolition is to be done by mechanical\
      \ means such as weight ball and power\\n                  shovels, the following additional precautions are\
      \ necessary:\\n\\n                                              i. The area shall be barricaded for a minimum\
      \ distance of 1 ½ times the\\n                            height of the wall.\\n\\n                      \
      \                         ii. The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades, which\\n   \
      \                            shall be of plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting,\\n \
      \                            with synthetic enamel paint of approved color, quality and brand, writing\\n\
      \                                  letter and logo of metro including maintenance of the same, duly cleaning\\\
      n                           the same frequently, arrangement for blinker light as per requirement.\\n    \
      \                      During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously. Removal of\\n           \
      \                barricade will not be permitted till completion of demolition, dismantling of\\n        \
      \                     properties\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September\
      \ 2026           Section Page 22 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n\

      : 8,\n      \"text\": \"explained. It shall be ensured that the workers are using all the safety appliances\

      : [\n        72.00053405761719,\n        238.56280517578125,\n        539.228759765625,\n        362.7981262207031\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"(1) \\nSafety helmets as per IS 2925: 1984; \\n(2)\
      \ \\nGoggles made of celluloid lens to be worn at the time of demolition of floors, \\nwalls, tearing of plaster,\
      \ etc., especially when equipment like jack hammers \\nare used for demolition work, to protect the eyes from\
      \ flying pieces, dust, \\ndirt, etc. that may be blown up by wind. \\n(3) \\nLeather or rubber gloves to be\
      \ worn during demolition of RCC work or \\nremoving steel work, where the hands of workers are likely to be\
      \ injured. \\n(4) \\nSafety belts while working at higher level to prevent falling from the \\nstructure.\
      \ \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        109.44051361083984,\n        365.0426025390625,\n \

      : \"(p) First-aid equipment shall be available at all demolition works of any magnitude. Also, \\n\"\n   \

      \ a qualified doctor be available at call. \\n(q) When there is a possibility of fire breaking out, appropriate\

      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"prevent possible damage, these walls shall be supported until such time as \\npermanent\

      : 14,\n      \"text\": \"rails shall extend not less than 1.0 m above the floor or platform to which the ladder\
      \ \\ngives access. All ladders shall be secured against slipping out at the bottom and \\nagainst movement\

      : \"a height not less than 1 m above the floor level. All floor openings and shafts not \\nmeant as material\

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 27,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"174\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n               roadway along the entire length of demolition\
      \ side of the property with movable bars\\n               as may be necessary for the proper prosecution of\
      \ the work.\\n                       l)  Where workers entrances to the building being demolished are not\
      \ completely\\n                 protected by sidewalk sheds, all such entrances shall be protected by canopies\\\
      n                extending from the face of the building to a point not less than 2.5 m from it. In such\\\
      n              a case, overhead projection shall be at least 0.6 m wider than the building entrance\\n   \
      \               or opening and every canopy shall be as strong as the sidewalk shed.\\n\\n             3.8\
      \   Sequence of demolition operations: The sequence of demolition shall generally\\n           be as given\
      \ below:\\n\\n              a) The demolition shall always proceed systematically storey by storey in descending\\\
      n             order and the demolition of upper floors shall be completely over before any of the\\n     \
      \        supporting members or other important portion on the lower floor  is disturbed. No\\n           \
      \ unnecessary work shall go on below when the demolition is in progress above. When\\n          some work\
      \ is to be done at the lower level, adequate protection shall be provided for all\\n             the workmen\
      \ so engaged.\\n              b) The requirements of (a) shall not prohibit the demolition of structures by\
      \ sections, if\\n          means are taken to prevent injuries to persons or damage to property.\\n      \
      \         c)     Roofs (or floors), generally, be demolished first before demolishing the supporting\\n  \
      \            walls structural elements.\\n              d)      All glazed sash, glazed doors and windows\
      \ etc., shall be removed before the\\n              demolition of roofs and walls starts. All fragile and\
      \ loose fixtures shall be removed. Lath\\n           and loose plaster be stripped off throughout the entire\
      \ structure. This is advantageous\\n           because it reduces glass breakage and also eliminates a large\
      \ amount of dust producing\\n              material before more substantial parts of the building are removed.\\\
      n              e) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades, which shall be of\\n   \
      \            plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic enamel paint\\n \
      \              of approved  color,  quality and brand,  writing  letter and logo  of metro  including\\n \
      \           maintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker\\n       \
      \          light as per requirement. During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously.\\n        \
      \   Removal of barricade will not be permitted  till completion of demolition, dismantling of\\n         \
      \     properties\\n\\n             3.9    Demolition of floors. For demolition of floors the following procedure\
      \ may be\\n               followed:\\n\\n              a) A slit in width not exceeding 300 mm shall be cut\
      \ at the first stage for the entire length\\n               of the slab along which it spans. The opening\
      \ shall thereafter be increased to the desired\\n             width by suitable installments.\\n         \
      \     b)     Planks of sufficient strength not less than 50 mm thick and 250 mm wide shall be\\n         \
      \    provided at a spacing not greater than 0.4 m. These planks shall be so placed as to give\\n         \
      \  workmen firm support to guard against any unexpected collapse.\\n               c)     Stringers of ample\
      \ strength shall be installed to support the planks where\\n            necessary and the ends of stringers\
      \ shall be supported by floor beams, girders and not\\n            by floor slab alone.\\n              d)\
      \    When floors are being removed, no workmen shall be allowed to work in the area,\\n                directly\
      \ underneath and such area shall be barricaded to prevent access to it.\\n              e)    The demolition\
      \ of the floor in question shall be started only after the surrounding\\n            area for a distance of\
      \ 6 m have been entirely cleared of persons, and the debris and other\\n            unnecessary material removed.\\\
      n                    f)     Planks used for temporary protection shall be sound and at least 50 mm thick.\\\
      n           They shall be laid close together with ends overlapping at least 100 mm over solid bearing\\n\
      \               to prevent tipping under load.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.\
      \                 September 2026           Section Page 24 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"\
      bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n        56.49034118652344\n\

      \ of the property with movable bars \\nas may be necessary for the proper prosecution of the work. \\nl) Where\

      \ be protected by canopies \\nextending from the face of the building to a point not less than 2.5 m from\
      \ it. In such \\na case, overhead projection shall be at least 0.6 m wider than the building entrance \\nor\
      \ opening and every canopy shall be as strong as the sidewalk shed. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        90.72013854980469,\n        175.2030029296875,\n        539.229248046875,\n        501.878173828125\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"3.8       Sequence of demolition operations: The\
      \ sequence of demolition shall generally \\nbe as given below: \\n \\na) The demolition shall always proceed\
      \ systematically storey by storey in descending \\norder and the demolition of upper floors shall be completely\
      \ over before any of the \\nsupporting members or other important portion on the lower floor is disturbed.\
      \ No \\nunnecessary work shall go on below when the demolition is in progress above. When \\nsome work is\
      \ to be done at the lower level, adequate protection shall be provided for all \\nthe workmen so engaged.\
      \ \\nb) The requirements of (a) shall not prohibit the demolition of structures by sections, if \\nmeans are\
      \ taken to prevent injuries to persons or damage to property. \\nc) \\nRoofs (or floors), generally, be demolished\
      \ first before demolishing the supporting \\nwalls structural elements. \\nd) \\nAll glazed sash, glazed doors\
      \ and windows etc., shall be removed before the \\ndemolition of roofs and walls starts. All fragile and loose\
      \ fixtures shall be removed. Lath \\nand loose plaster be stripped off throughout the entire structure. This\
      \ is advantageous \\nbecause it reduces glass breakage and also eliminates a large amount of dust producing\
      \ \\nmaterial before more substantial parts of the building are removed. \\ne) The structure to be demolished/dismantled\
      \ must have barricades, which shall be of \\nplain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting,\
      \ with synthetic enamel paint \\nof approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including\
      \ \\nmaintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker \\nlight as per requirement.\
      \ During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously. \\nRemoval of barricade will not be permitted\
      \ till completion of demolition, dismantling of \\nproperties \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        108.00009155273438,\n        504.1226806640625,\n        542.347900390625,\n        754.9581298828125\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"3.9  \\nDemolition of floors. For demolition of floors\
      \ the following procedure may be \\nfollowed: \\n \\na) A slit in width not exceeding 300 mm shall be cut\
      \ at the first stage for the entire length \\nof the slab along which it spans.  The opening shall thereafter\
      \ be increased to the desired \\nwidth by suitable installments. \\nb) \\nPlanks of sufficient strength not\
      \ less than 50 mm thick and 250 mm wide shall be \\nprovided at a spacing not greater than 0.4 m.  These planks\
      \ shall be so placed as to give \\nworkmen firm support to guard against any unexpected collapse. \\nc) \\\
      nStringers of ample strength shall be installed to support the planks where \\nnecessary and the ends of stringers\
      \ shall be supported by floor beams, girders and not \\nby floor slab alone. \\nd) \\nWhen floors are being\
      \ removed, no workmen shall be allowed to work in the area, \\ndirectly underneath and such area shall be\
      \ barricaded to prevent access to it. \\ne) \\nThe demolition of the floor in question shall be started only\
      \ after the surrounding \\narea for a distance of 6 m have been entirely cleared of persons, and the debris\
      \ and other \\nunnecessary material removed. \\nf) \\nPlanks used for temporary protection shall be sound\
      \ and at least 50 mm thick.  \\nThey shall be laid close together with ends overlapping at least 100 mm over\
      \ solid bearing \\nto prevent tipping under load. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        304.079833984375,\n\
      \        757.20263671875,\n        307.1471252441406,\n        767.5581665039062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\

- kind: parameter
  text: Sidewalk sheds constructed for public and pedestrian protection must be capable of sustaining a live load
    of 730 kg/m2 (or 1460 kg/m2 if material storage is exceptionally permitted), with maximum operational load restricted
    to 600 kg/m2 and a minimum headroom clearance of 2.4 m.

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 26,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"173\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n\\n                                   iii.  While the mechanical\
      \ device is in operation no workmen shall be allowed\\n                       to enter the building being\
      \ demolished;\\n\\n                        iv. The device shall be so located as to avoid falling debris;\
      \ and\\n\\n                     v. The device when being used shall not cause any damage to adjacent\\n  \
      \                      structure, power line, other services, etc.\\n\\n       3.7    Protection of the public\\\
      n\\n       Protection of the public before and during demolition is important and the following points\\n\
      \       should be kept in mind:\\n\\n       a) Every sidewalk or road adjacent to the work shall be closed\
      \ or protected. All main\\n           roads, which are open to the public shall be kept open to the public\
      \ clear and\\n          unobstructed at all times.\\n       b)  Children and public shall be kept out of the\
      \ building and the adjoining yards.\\n        c)   If the structure to be demolished is more than two-storied\
      \ or 7.5 m high, measured\\n          from the sidewalk or street which cannot be closed or safely diverted,\
      \ and the\\n           horizontal distance from the inside edge of the side walk to the structure is 4.5 m\
      \ or\\n            less, a substantial side walk shed shall be constructed over the entire length of the\\\
      n          sidewalk adjacent to the structure of sufficient width with a view to accommodating\\n        \
      \   the pedestrian  traffic without causing congestion. The sidewalk shall be lighted\\n             sufficiently\
      \ to ensure safety at all times.\\n       d) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades,\
      \ which shall be of\\n            plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic\
      \ enamel\\n           paint of approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including\\\
      n         maintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker\\n         \
      \    light as per requirement. During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously.\\n         Removal\
      \ of barricade will not be permitted till completion of demolition, dismantling\\n            of properties.\\\

- kind: parameter
  text: Catch platforms for exterior wall demolition in multi-storey buildings must be at least 1.5 m wide, supportable
    on outriggers spaced up to 3 m apart, equipped with a continuous solid 1 m high parapet, and capable of sustaining
    a live load of not less than 610 kg/m2.

      \                      structure, power line, other services, etc.\\n\\n       3.7    Protection of the public\\\
      n\\n       Protection of the public before and during demolition is important and the following points\\n\
      \       should be kept in mind:\\n\\n       a) Every sidewalk or road adjacent to the work shall be closed\
      \ or protected. All main\\n           roads, which are open to the public shall be kept open to the public\
      \ clear and\\n          unobstructed at all times.\\n       b)  Children and public shall be kept out of the\
      \ building and the adjoining yards.\\n        c)   If the structure to be demolished is more than two-storied\
      \ or 7.5 m high, measured\\n          from the sidewalk or street which cannot be closed or safely diverted,\
      \ and the\\n           horizontal distance from the inside edge of the side walk to the structure is 4.5 m\
      \ or\\n            less, a substantial side walk shed shall be constructed over the entire length of the\\\
      n          sidewalk adjacent to the structure of sufficient width with a view to accommodating\\n        \
      \   the pedestrian  traffic without causing congestion. The sidewalk shall be lighted\\n             sufficiently\
      \ to ensure safety at all times.\\n       d) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades,\

      n         maintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker\\n         \
      \    light as per requirement. During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously.\\n         Removal\

- kind: summary
  text: 'Mandatory worker safety appliances include safety helmets (IS 2925: 1984), celluloid lens goggles for dust
    and flying debris during jackhammer operations, leather or rubber gloves for RCC and steel removal, and safety
    belts for high-level work.'
  evidence_ids:
  - Jcc4871ddd5018819
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 25,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"172\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n                     (j)  Construction sheds and toolboxes\
      \ should be so located as to protect workers from\\n                    injuries of falling objects, wall,\
      \ etc.\\n                (k) A warning device should be installed in the area to be used to warn the workers,\
      \ in\\n               case of danger.\\n                     (l)  Screens shall be placed, where necessary,\
      \ to prevent flying pieces from injuring the\\n                   fellow workmen.\\n            (m) No demolition\
      \ work shall be carried out during storm or heavy rain.\\n               (n) No demolition work shall be carried\
      \ out at night especially when the building or\\n                   structure to be demolished is in an inhabited\
      \ area.\\n               (o)  All necessary safety appliances shall be issued to the workmen and their use\\\
      n                  explained. It shall be ensured that the workers are using all the safety appliances\\n\
      \                  while at work. The safety appliances should be as follows:\\n\\n                     (1)\
      \    Safety helmets as per IS 2925: 1984;\\n                     (2)    Goggles made of celluloid lens to\
      \ be worn at the time of demolition of floors,\\n                             walls, tearing of plaster, etc.,\
      \ especially when equipment like jack hammers\\n                         are used for demolition work, to\
      \ protect the eyes from flying pieces, dust,\\n                                    dirt, etc. that may be\
      \ blown up by wind.\\n                     (3)    Leather or rubber gloves to be worn during demolition of\
      \ RCC work or\\n                       removing steel work, where the hands of workers are likely to be injured.\\\
      n                     (4)    Safety belts while working  at higher level to prevent  falling from the\\n \
      \                            structure.\\n\\n                (p) First-aid equipment shall be available at\
      \ all demolition works of any magnitude. Also,\\n               by prior arrangement, a qualified doctor be\
      \ available at call.\\n               (q) When there is a possibility of fire breaking out, appropriate portable\
      \ first-aid fire\\n                 appliances (see IS 2190: 1992) shall be kept at hand.\\n             \
      \     (r)  The removal of a member may weaken the side wall of an adjoining structure and to\\n          \
      \       prevent possible damage, these walls  shall be supported  until such time as\\n               permanent\
      \ protection is provided.  In case of any danger is anticipated to the\\n                  adjoining structure,\
      \ the same shall be got vacated to avoid any danger to human life.\\n                (s)  Ladders, when used,\
      \ shall conform to IS 3696 (Part 2): 1991. Ladders or their side\\n                      rails shall extend\
      \ not less than 1.0 m above the floor or platform to which the ladder\\n                 gives access. All\
      \ ladders shall be secured against slipping out at the bottom and\\n                  against movement in\
      \ any direction at the top.\\n                   (t)   All exterior wall openings which extend down to the\
      \ floor level shall be barricaded to\\n               a height not less than 1 m above the floor level. All\
      \ floor openings and shafts not\\n               meant as material chutes shall be floored over and endorsed\
      \ with ground rails and\\n                 toe boards.\\n               (u)  All existing fixtures/services\
      \ required during demolition operations shall be well\\n                 protected with substantial covering\
      \ to the satisfaction of the Authority.\\n                (v) When demolition is to be done by mechanical\
      \ means such as weight ball and power\\n                  shovels, the following additional precautions are\
      \ necessary:\\n\\n                                              i. The area shall be barricaded for a minimum\
      \ distance of 1 ½ times the\\n                            height of the wall.\\n\\n                      \
      \                         ii. The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades, which\\n   \
      \                            shall be of plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting,\\n \
      \                            with synthetic enamel paint of approved color, quality and brand, writing\\n\
      \                                  letter and logo of metro including maintenance of the same, duly cleaning\\\
      n                           the same frequently, arrangement for blinker light as per requirement.\\n    \
      \                      During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously. Removal of\\n           \
      \                barricade will not be permitted till completion of demolition, dismantling of\\n        \
      \                     properties\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September\
      \ 2026           Section Page 22 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n\

- kind: summary
  text: Part 4A describes systematic demolition storey by storey in descending order. Its floor-demolition procedure
    includes an initial slab slit not exceeding 300 mm, planks at least 50 mm thick and 250 mm wide spaced not more
    than 0.4 m apart, and clearance of persons from the surrounding area for a distance of 6 m before that floor’s
    demolition starts, with debris and unnecessary material removed.
  evidence_ids:
  - J0e5cc32bd4d8850a
  - Jeceaf38f02c9478f
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 28,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"175\\n BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \              Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n       3.10   Demolition of walls\\n\\n       3.10.1 Procedure.\
      \   The following procedure shall be followed when demolishing walls:\\n\\n        a) While walls or sections\
      \ of masonry are being demolished  it shall be ensured that\\n           they are not allowed to fall as a\
      \ single mass on the floors of the building so as not to\\n          exceed the safe carrying capacity of\
      \ the floor; wherever practicable, they may fall\\n         away from the floors on to catch platforms. Overloading\
      \ of floors shall be prevented\\n          by removing the accumulating debris through chutes or by other\
      \ means immediately.\\n         The floor shall be inspected by the Authority before undertaking demolition\
      \ work and\\n                     if the same is found incapable of carrying the load of debris, necessary\
      \ precautions\\n             shall be taken to prevent any unexpected collapse of the floor.\\n        b)\
      \  Walls shall be removed part by part. Stages shall be provided for the men to work\\n           on, if the\
      \ walls are very thin and dangerous to work by standing over them.\\n         c) No section of the wall whose\
      \ height is more than 15 times the thickness, shall be\\n           permitted to stand without lateral bracing\
      \ unless such a wall is in good condition and\\n         was originally designed to stand without such lateral\
      \ bracing or support.\\n        d)  Structural or load supporting members on any floor shall not be removed\
      \ or cut until\\n                all the storey above that floor have been demolished and removed.\\n    \
      \    e)  Before demolishing any interior or exterior wall within 3 m of the opening in the floor\\n      \
      \     immediately below, such opening shall be substantially planked over, unless access\\n              \
      \ is denied to workmen to that portion of the floor immediately below the opening, in\\n           the floor\
      \ of the storey being demolished, where any debris passing through the\\n          opening may fall.\\n  \
      \          f)  In framed structures, if the structure is properly designed to the standards and to the\\n\
      \            specifications in such cases the frame may be left in position during demolition of\\n      \
      \    masonry work. Where this is done all beams, girders, etc., shall be cleared of all\\n           loose\
      \ materials as the demolition of masonry work progresses downward provided it\\n               is still strong\
      \ enough to stand as an independent structure.\\n\\n        g) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must\
      \ have barricades, which shall be of\\n             plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting,\
      \ with synthetic enamel\\n            paint of approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo\
      \ of metro including\\n          maintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for\
      \ blinker\\n              light as per requirement. During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously.\\\
      n         Removal of barricade will not be permitted till completion of demolition, dismantling\\n       \
      \      of properties.\\n        h) Walkways shall be provided to enable workmen to reach or leave their work\
      \ on any\\n            scaffold or wall. Such walkways shall neither be less than 3 planks wide, nor less\\\
      n           than 0.8 m in width.\\n             i)   After completion of each day’s work, all walls shall\
      \ be left stable to avoid any danger\\n             of getting overturned.\\n             j)  Foundation walls\
      \ which serve as retaining walls to support the earth or adjoining\\n             structure, shall not be\
      \ demolished until such an adjoining structure has been under\\n           pinned or braced and the earth\
      \ removed by sheet piling or sheathing.\\n\\n       3.10.2 Catch platforms\\n      Catch platforms shall be\
      \ provided in case of demolition of exterior walls in multi-storey\\n        buildings. The following details\
      \ may be considered:\\n\\n       a) Catch platforms shall generally be provided for multi-storied buildings\
      \ more than 20\\n    m high to prevent injuries to the worker and to the public when exterior walls are being\\\
      n       demolished.\\n       b)    Such platforms shall be constructed and maintained not more than three\
      \ storey\\n      below the storey from which the exterior wall is being demolished. When demolition has\\\
      n\\n\\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September 2026           Section Page 25 of 37\"\
      ,\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n\
      \        526.5408935546875,\n        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157              Employer Requirement, Scope of Work & \
      \ \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n\
      \        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section\
      \ Page 25 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n\
      \        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00001525878906,\n        74.04320526123047,\n\

      \        99.36315155029297,\n        539.2274169921875,\n        122.43863677978516\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 4,\n      \"text\": \"3.10.1 Procedure. \\nThe following procedure shall be followed when demolishing walls:\
      \ \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.56028747558594,\n        124.80309295654297,\n\
      \        539.2272338867188,\n        135.1586151123047\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"a) While walls or sections of masonry are being demolished it shall be ensured that \\n\"\n    },\n  \
      \  {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.56031799316406,\n        137.403076171875,\n        542.3474731445312,\n\
      \        236.31834411621094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"they are not allowed\
      \ to fall as a single mass on the floors of the building so as not to \\nexceed the safe carrying capacity\
      \ of the floor; wherever practicable, they may fall \\naway from the floors on to catch platforms. Overloading\
      \ of floors shall be prevented \\nby removing the accumulating debris through chutes or by other means immediately.\
      \  \\nThe floor shall be inspected by the Authority before undertaking demolition work and \\nif the same\
      \ is found incapable of carrying the load of debris, necessary precautions \\nshall be taken to prevent any\
      \ unexpected collapse of the floor. \\nb) Walls shall be removed part by part. Stages shall be provided for\
      \ the men to work \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.56028747558594,\n        238.56280517578125,\n\
      \        539.2276000976562,\n        261.6382751464844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 7,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"on, if the walls are very thin and dangerous to work by standing over them. \\nc) No section of the wall\
      \ whose height is more than 15 times the thickness, shall be \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n   \
      \     112.56028747558594,\n        263.88275146484375,\n        539.2278442382812,\n        299.5581970214844\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"permitted to stand without lateral bracing unless\
      \ such a wall is in good condition and \\nwas originally designed to stand without such lateral bracing or\
      \ support. \\nd) Structural or load supporting members on any floor shall not be removed or cut until \\n\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.56028747558594,\n        301.80267333984375,\n        539.2276611328125,\n\
      \        324.8781433105469\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"all the storey above that\
      \ floor have been demolished and removed. \\ne) Before demolishing any interior or exterior wall within 3\
      \ m of the opening in the floor \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.56047058105469,\n   \
      \     327.12261962890625,\n        539.2295532226562,\n        388.11810302734375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 10,\n      \"text\": \"immediately below, such opening shall be substantially planked over, unless access\
      \ \\nis denied to workmen to that portion of the floor immediately below the opening, in \\nthe floor of the\
      \ storey being demolished, where any debris passing through the \\nopening may fall. \\nf) In framed structures,\
      \ if the structure is properly designed to the standards and to the \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\":\
      \ [\n        108.00022888183594,\n        390.36260986328125,\n        539.228271484375,\n        451.35809326171875\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"specifications in such cases the frame may be left\
      \ in position during demolition of \\nmasonry work.  Where this is done all beams, girders, etc., shall be\
      \ cleared of all \\nloose materials as the demolition of masonry work progresses downward provided it \\nis\
      \ still strong enough to stand as an independent structure. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        112.56022644042969,\n        453.60260009765625,\n        539.228759765625,\n        463.9580993652344\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"g) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must\
      \ have barricades, which shall be of \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.55940246582031,\n\
      \        466.3226013183594,\n        539.2269897460938,\n        552.5181274414062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic enamel\
      \ \\npaint of approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including \\nmaintenance\
      \ of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker \\nlight as per requirement. During\
      \ demolition barricades has to be kept continuously. \\nRemoval of barricade will not be permitted till completion\
      \ of demolition, dismantling \\nof properties. \\nh) Walkways shall be provided to enable workmen to reach\
      \ or leave their work on any \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.55859375,\n        554.7625732421875,\n\
      \        539.2260131835938,\n        590.4381103515625\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 14,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"scaffold or wall. Such walkways shall neither be less than 3 planks wide, nor less \\nthan 0.8 m in width.\
      \ \\ni) After completion of each day’s work, all walls shall be left stable to avoid any danger \\n\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        112.5523910522461,\n        592.8026123046875,\n        539.2252197265625,\n\
      \        615.7581176757812\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"of getting overturned.\
      \ \\nj) Foundation walls which serve as retaining walls to support the earth or adjoining \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        130.55760192871094,\n        618.12255859375,\n        539.2243041992188,\n\
      \        641.078125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"structure, shall not be demolished\
      \ until such an adjoining structure has been under \\npinned or braced and the earth removed by sheet piling\
      \ or sheathing. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99748229980469,\n        643.3225708007812,\n\
      \        539.2250366210938,\n        767.55810546875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 17,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n3.10.2 Catch platforms  \\nCatch platforms shall be provided in case of demolition of exterior walls\
      \ in multi-storey \\nbuildings. The following details may be considered: \\n \\na) Catch platforms shall generally\
      \ be provided for multi-storied buildings more than 20 \\nm high to prevent injuries to the worker and to\
      \ the public when exterior walls are being \\ndemolished. \\nb) \\nSuch platforms shall be constructed and\
      \ maintained not more than three storey \\nbelow the storey from which the exterior wall is being demolished.\
      \  When demolition has \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n\
      \        570.0199584960938,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 18,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"175\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 27,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"174\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n               roadway along the entire length of demolition\
      \ side of the property with movable bars\\n               as may be necessary for the proper prosecution of\
      \ the work.\\n                       l)  Where workers entrances to the building being demolished are not\
      \ completely\\n                 protected by sidewalk sheds, all such entrances shall be protected by canopies\\\
      n                extending from the face of the building to a point not less than 2.5 m from it. In such\\\
      n              a case, overhead projection shall be at least 0.6 m wider than the building entrance\\n   \
      \               or opening and every canopy shall be as strong as the sidewalk shed.\\n\\n             3.8\
      \   Sequence of demolition operations: The sequence of demolition shall generally\\n           be as given\
      \ below:\\n\\n              a) The demolition shall always proceed systematically storey by storey in descending\\\
      n             order and the demolition of upper floors shall be completely over before any of the\\n     \
      \        supporting members or other important portion on the lower floor  is disturbed. No\\n           \
      \ unnecessary work shall go on below when the demolition is in progress above. When\\n          some work\
      \ is to be done at the lower level, adequate protection shall be provided for all\\n             the workmen\
      \ so engaged.\\n              b) The requirements of (a) shall not prohibit the demolition of structures by\
      \ sections, if\\n          means are taken to prevent injuries to persons or damage to property.\\n      \
      \         c)     Roofs (or floors), generally, be demolished first before demolishing the supporting\\n  \
      \            walls structural elements.\\n              d)      All glazed sash, glazed doors and windows\
      \ etc., shall be removed before the\\n              demolition of roofs and walls starts. All fragile and\
      \ loose fixtures shall be removed. Lath\\n           and loose plaster be stripped off throughout the entire\
      \ structure. This is advantageous\\n           because it reduces glass breakage and also eliminates a large\
      \ amount of dust producing\\n              material before more substantial parts of the building are removed.\\\
      n              e) The structure to be demolished/dismantled must have barricades, which shall be of\\n   \
      \            plain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting, with synthetic enamel paint\\n \
      \              of approved  color,  quality and brand,  writing  letter and logo  of metro  including\\n \
      \           maintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker\\n       \
      \          light as per requirement. During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously.\\n        \
      \   Removal of barricade will not be permitted  till completion of demolition, dismantling of\\n         \
      \     properties\\n\\n             3.9    Demolition of floors. For demolition of floors the following procedure\
      \ may be\\n               followed:\\n\\n              a) A slit in width not exceeding 300 mm shall be cut\
      \ at the first stage for the entire length\\n               of the slab along which it spans. The opening\
      \ shall thereafter be increased to the desired\\n             width by suitable installments.\\n         \
      \     b)     Planks of sufficient strength not less than 50 mm thick and 250 mm wide shall be\\n         \
      \    provided at a spacing not greater than 0.4 m. These planks shall be so placed as to give\\n         \
      \  workmen firm support to guard against any unexpected collapse.\\n               c)     Stringers of ample\
      \ strength shall be installed to support the planks where\\n            necessary and the ends of stringers\
      \ shall be supported by floor beams, girders and not\\n            by floor slab alone.\\n              d)\
      \    When floors are being removed, no workmen shall be allowed to work in the area,\\n                directly\
      \ underneath and such area shall be barricaded to prevent access to it.\\n              e)    The demolition\
      \ of the floor in question shall be started only after the surrounding\\n            area for a distance of\
      \ 6 m have been entirely cleared of persons, and the debris and other\\n            unnecessary material removed.\\\
      n                    f)     Planks used for temporary protection shall be sound and at least 50 mm thick.\\\
      n           They shall be laid close together with ends overlapping at least 100 mm over solid bearing\\n\
      \               to prevent tipping under load.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.\
      \                 September 2026           Section Page 24 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"\
      bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n        56.49034118652344\n\

      \ of the property with movable bars \\nas may be necessary for the proper prosecution of the work. \\nl) Where\

      : [\n        90.72005462646484,\n        112.08312225341797,\n        539.2275390625,\n        173.0785369873047\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"protected by sidewalk sheds, all such entrances shall\
      \ be protected by canopies \\nextending from the face of the building to a point not less than 2.5 m from\
      \ it. In such \\na case, overhead projection shall be at least 0.6 m wider than the building entrance \\nor\
      \ opening and every canopy shall be as strong as the sidewalk shed. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        90.72013854980469,\n        175.2030029296875,\n        539.229248046875,\n        501.878173828125\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"3.8       Sequence of demolition operations: The\
      \ sequence of demolition shall generally \\nbe as given below: \\n \\na) The demolition shall always proceed\
      \ systematically storey by storey in descending \\norder and the demolition of upper floors shall be completely\
      \ over before any of the \\nsupporting members or other important portion on the lower floor is disturbed.\
      \ No \\nunnecessary work shall go on below when the demolition is in progress above. When \\nsome work is\
      \ to be done at the lower level, adequate protection shall be provided for all \\nthe workmen so engaged.\
      \ \\nb) The requirements of (a) shall not prohibit the demolition of structures by sections, if \\nmeans are\
      \ taken to prevent injuries to persons or damage to property. \\nc) \\nRoofs (or floors), generally, be demolished\
      \ first before demolishing the supporting \\nwalls structural elements. \\nd) \\nAll glazed sash, glazed doors\
      \ and windows etc., shall be removed before the \\ndemolition of roofs and walls starts. All fragile and loose\
      \ fixtures shall be removed. Lath \\nand loose plaster be stripped off throughout the entire structure. This\
      \ is advantageous \\nbecause it reduces glass breakage and also eliminates a large amount of dust producing\
      \ \\nmaterial before more substantial parts of the building are removed. \\ne) The structure to be demolished/dismantled\
      \ must have barricades, which shall be of \\nplain MS Sheet of 16 gauge fixed with steel frame, painting,\
      \ with synthetic enamel paint \\nof approved color, quality and brand, writing letter and logo of metro including\
      \ \\nmaintenance of the same, duly cleaning the same frequently, arrangement for blinker \\nlight as per requirement.\
      \ During demolition barricades has to be kept continuously. \\nRemoval of barricade will not be permitted\
      \ till completion of demolition, dismantling of \\nproperties \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        108.00009155273438,\n        504.1226806640625,\n        542.347900390625,\n        754.9581298828125\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"3.9  \\nDemolition of floors. For demolition of floors\
      \ the following procedure may be \\nfollowed: \\n \\na) A slit in width not exceeding 300 mm shall be cut\
      \ at the first stage for the entire length \\nof the slab along which it spans.  The opening shall thereafter\
      \ be increased to the desired \\nwidth by suitable installments. \\nb) \\nPlanks of sufficient strength not\
      \ less than 50 mm thick and 250 mm wide shall be \\nprovided at a spacing not greater than 0.4 m.  These planks\
      \ shall be so placed as to give \\nworkmen firm support to guard against any unexpected collapse. \\nc) \\\
      nStringers of ample strength shall be installed to support the planks where \\nnecessary and the ends of stringers\
      \ shall be supported by floor beams, girders and not \\nby floor slab alone. \\nd) \\nWhen floors are being\
      \ removed, no workmen shall be allowed to work in the area, \\ndirectly underneath and such area shall be\
      \ barricaded to prevent access to it. \\ne) \\nThe demolition of the floor in question shall be started only\
      \ after the surrounding \\narea for a distance of 6 m have been entirely cleared of persons, and the debris\
      \ and other \\nunnecessary material removed. \\nf) \\nPlanks used for temporary protection shall be sound\
      \ and at least 50 mm thick.  \\nThey shall be laid close together with ends overlapping at least 100 mm over\
      \ solid bearing \\nto prevent tipping under load. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        304.079833984375,\n\
      \        757.20263671875,\n        307.1471252441406,\n        767.5581665039062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 7,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n\

- kind: summary
  text: Volume 2 Part 4A specifies detailed operational procedures, public protection measures, catch platform requirements,
    and element-specific demolition sequences for masonry, concrete, steel, and timber structures.
  evidence_ids:

    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol2/vol2_part4A.md
    okf_sha256: 520ac8689dbcfe1f3be2ed99dafda8715eb436313221c6ca77dd66080cd2f726
    heading: 'Employer Requirement, Scope of Work and Technical Specifications – Part 4A: Specifications for Demolition/Dismantling
      Works'
    source_document: Vol2DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: e44cc4945aec704af8350cca6388b3fe3b741d76577f7da70cee6a836662582b
    pdf_pages:

    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol2/part_4a_demolition/demolition_clauses.json
    json_sha256: e15324023aacf8bda736e3cf21e3a65735deb4ba19d458a5cd2099534b681a2e

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"pdf_page\": 29,\n\
      \  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"176\\n       BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \       Employer Requirement, Scope of Work &\\n                                                         \
      \                     Technical Specifications\\n\\n\\n            progressed to within three storey of ground\
      \ level, catch platforms will not be considered\\n             necessary.\\n               c)     Catch platforms\
      \ shall not be less than 1.5 m in width measured in a horizontal\\n               direction from the face\
      \ of the structure and shall consist of outriggers supported not more\\n             than 3 m apart. Planks\
      \ shall be laid tight together, without openings between them and\\n             the walls. Catch platforms\
      \ shall be provided with a continuous solid parapet along its\\n              outer edge of at least 1 m height.\
      \ The parapet may be constructed with the same material\\n            as the platform.\\n              d)\
      \ Catch platform shall be capable of sustaining a live load of not less than 610 kg/m2.\\n              e)\
      \ Catch platforms shall neither be used for storing of materials nor dumping of\\n                  materials.\\\
      n\\n             3.11   Demolition of different types of structures and elements\\n\\n             3.11.1\
      \ General.      Structures may be dealt with as masonry, concrete, steel and\\n              timber. The structures\
      \ or their elements shall be dealt with as below, in addition to other\\n             requirements as applicable.\\\
      n\\n            3.11.2 Masonry structures.  Jack Arches – Where tie rods are present between main\\n     \
      \       supporting beams, these should not be cut until after the arch or series of arches in the\\n     \
      \          floor have been removed. Particular care should be exercised and full examination of\\n       \
      \      structure be made before the demolition is commenced. The floor should be demolished\\n           \
      \    in strips parallel to the span of arch rings (at right angles to the main floor beam).\\n\\n\\n\\n  \
      \          Brick Arches\\n\\n              1)   As much dead load as possible may be removed provided it does\
      \ not interfere with\\n                 stability of main arch rings; it should be noted that the load carrying\
      \ capacity of many old\\n             arches relies on the filling between the spandrels. On no account should\
      \ the restraining\\n              influence of the abutments be removed before the dead load of the spandrel\
      \ fill and the\\n             arch rings are removed. The normal sequence of demolition includes the following:\\\
      n                           -    remove the spandrel filling down to the springing line,\\n              \
      \             -    remove the arch rings,\\n                           -    Remove the abutments.\\n\\n  \
      \            2)   A single span arch can be demolished, by hand, by cutting narrow segments\\n           \
      \   progressively from each springing parallel to the span of the arch, until the width of the\\n        \
      \     arch has been reduced to a minimum which can then collapse. Where it is impossible to\\n           \
      \   allow debris to fall to the ground below, centering designed to carry the load should be\\n          \
      \   erected and the arch demolished progressively. The design of the centering should make\\n            \
      \  appropriate allowance for impact.\\n              3)    Where  deliberate collapse  is  feasible the crown\
      \ may be broken working\\n              progressively from the edges to the center.\\n              4)   \
      \  In multi-span arches, before individual spans are removed, lateral restraint should\\n           be provided\
      \ at the springing level. Demolition may be proceeded as for a single span care\\n             being taken\
      \ to demolish the spandrels down to the springing line as the work proceeds.\\n\\n             3.11.3 Reinforced\
      \ concrete\\n\\n              a)  Before commencing demolition, the condition and position of reinforcement\
      \ and\\n                    possibility of lack of  its continuity should be ascertained. Demolition should\
      \ be\\n             commenced by removing partitions, non-load bearing cladding, etc. and similar non-\\n\
      \                   structural elements.\\n              b) Where hand demolition methods are used, the following\
      \ procedures should be used:\\n\\n\\n       Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                 September\
      \ 2026           Section Page 26 of 37\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99940490722656,\n\
      \        37.728336334228516,\n        526.5408935546875,\n        56.49034118652344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157              Employer Requirement,\
      \ Scope of Work &  \\n \\n \\n Technical Specifications \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n\
      \        787.1278076171875,\n        538.54248046875,\n        795.56982421875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 1,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd. \\n \\nSeptember 2026                    Section\
      \ Page 26 of 37 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        798.4948120117188,\n\
      \        538.4083251953125,\n        826.9197998046875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99992370605469,\n        74.16320037841797,\n\
      \        539.2275390625,\n        198.39842224121094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"progressed to within three storey of ground level, catch platforms will not be considered \\nnecessary.\
      \ \\nc) \\nCatch platforms shall not be less than 1.5 m in width measured in a horizontal \\ndirection from\
      \ the face of the structure and shall consist of outriggers supported not more \\nthan 3 m apart.  Planks\
      \ shall be laid tight together, without openings between them and \\nthe walls.  Catch platforms shall be\
      \ provided with a continuous solid parapet along its \\nouter edge of at least 1 m height. The parapet may\
      \ be constructed with the same material \\nas the platform. \\nd) Catch platform shall be capable of sustaining\
      \ a live load of not less than 610 kg/m2. \\ne) Catch platforms shall neither be used for storing of materials\
      \ nor dumping of \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00016784667969,\n        200.64288330078125,\n\
      \        176.7073974609375,\n        223.59837341308594\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"materials. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99966430664062,\n        225.84283447265625,\n\
      \        439.8668518066406,\n        248.79832458496094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"3.11 \\nDemolition of different types of structures and elements \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        71.99967956542969,\n        251.16278076171875,\n        539.2271118164062,\n        299.5581970214844\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"3.11.1 General. \\nStructures may be dealt with as\
      \ masonry, concrete, steel and \\ntimber. The structures or their elements shall be dealt with as below, in\
      \ addition to other \\nrequirements as applicable. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        103.55964660644531,\n\
      \        301.68267822265625,\n        539.2273559570312,\n        362.7981872558594\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 7,\n      \"text\": \"3.11.2 Masonry structures. Jack Arches – Where tie rods are present between main \\\
      nsupporting beams, these should not be cut until after the arch or series of arches in the \\nfloor have been\
      \ removed. Particular care should be exercised and full examination of \\nstructure be made before the demolition\
      \ is commenced. The floor should be demolished \\nin strips parallel to the span of arch rings (at right angles\
      \ to the main floor beam). \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99897766113281,\n        365.04266357421875,\n\
      \        539.2257080078125,\n        476.67816162109375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n \\nBrick Arches  \\n \\n1) \\nAs much dead load as possible may be removed provided it does not interfere\
      \ with \\nstability of main arch rings; it should be noted that the load carrying capacity of many old \\\
      narches relies on the filling between the spandrels. On no account should the restraining \\ninfluence of\
      \ the abutments be removed before the dead load of the spandrel fill and the \\narch rings are removed. The\
      \ normal sequence of demolition includes the following: \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99861145019531,\n\
      \        478.92266845703125,\n        539.2261352539062,\n        628.4782104492188\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 9,\n      \"text\": \"- \\nremove the spandrel filling down to the springing line, \\n- \\nremove the arch\
      \ rings, \\n- \\nRemove the abutments. \\n \\n2) \\nA single span arch can be demolished, by hand, by cutting\
      \ narrow segments \\nprogressively from each springing parallel to the span of the arch, until the width of\
      \ the \\narch has been reduced to a minimum which can then collapse.  Where it is impossible to \\nallow debris\
      \ to fall to the ground below, centering designed to carry the load should be \\nerected and the arch demolished\
      \ progressively. The design of the centering should make \\nappropriate allowance for impact. \\n3) \\nWhere\
      \ deliberate collapse is feasible the crown may be broken working \\nprogressively from the edges to the center.\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.9990234375,\n        630.72265625,\n        540.9058227539062,\n\
      \        678.9981689453125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 10,\n      \"text\": \"4) \\nIn multi-span arches,\
      \ before individual spans are removed, lateral restraint should \\nbe provided at the springing level. Demolition\
      \ may be proceeded as for a single span care \\nbeing taken to demolish the spandrels down to the springing\
      \ line as the work proceeds.   \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.9990234375,\n     \
      \   681.24267578125,\n        253.3863525390625,\n        704.1981811523438\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 11,\n      \"text\": \"3.11.3  Reinforced concrete \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n       \
      \ 107.27899932861328,\n        706.5626220703125,\n        539.2261962890625,\n        716.9181518554688\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"a) Before commencing demolition, the condition and\
      \ position of reinforcement and \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        107.99876403808594,\n   \
      \     719.2826538085938,\n        539.2260131835938,\n        767.5581665039062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"possibility of lack of its continuity should be ascertained. Demolition should be\
      \ \\ncommenced by removing partitions, non-load bearing cladding, etc. and similar non-\\nstructural elements.\
      \ \\nb) Where hand demolition methods are used, the following procedures should be used: \\n\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n\
      \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"176\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n\
      }"
